Due to the continuous emergence of FL algorithms and the highly heterogeneous nature of medical data, objectively evaluating their performance in real-world clinical settings remains difficult. Therefore, a comprehensive federated medical imaging benchmark, serving as a unified evaluation standard, is crucial for advancing the technology toward reliable clinical application. Existing federated medical imaging benchmarks have not yet adequately incorporated state-of-the-art algorithms, are limited to data from single organs or modalities, and overly emphasize model accuracy, making it difficult to comprehensively assess the overall efficacy of FL in real-world medical environments. To address these challenges, we developed the MobenFL benchmark. This benchmark integrates 20 cutting-edge FL algorithms and 22 medical imaging datasets, covering 12 critical organs across the human body, surpassing existing benchmark in breadth. In terms of evaluation dimensions, MobenFL not only assesses performance but also systematically incorporates key metrics such as algorithmic efficiency and privacy protection capabilities. Additionally, it conducts specialized evaluations for complex real-world clinical scenarios involving different diseases, devices, and imaging modalities, thereby providing a comprehensive and in-depth evaluation framework for the clinical application of FL in the medical field.

中文摘要翻译: 医疗数据的隐私要求及其在器官和模态上的巨大差异阻碍了医学人工智能的临床应用。联邦学习（FL）是克服这些挑战的一种可行方法。由于FL算法的不断涌现以及医学数据的高度异质性，在真实临床环境中客观评估其性能仍然困难。因此，一个全面的联邦医学影像基准，作为统一的评估标准，对于推动该技术走向可靠的临床应用至关重要。现有的联邦医学影像基准尚未充分纳入最先进的算法，局限于单一器官或模态的数据，并且过度强调模型准确性，难以全面评估FL在真实医疗环境中的整体效能。为应对这些挑战，我们开发了MobenFL基准。该基准集成了20种前沿FL算法和22个医学影像数据集，覆盖人体12个关键器官，在广度上超越了现有基准。在评估维度上，MobenFL不仅评估模型性能，还系统性地纳入了算法效率和隐私保护能力等关键指标。此外，它还针对涉及不同疾病、设备和成像模态的复杂真实临床场景进行专项评估，从而为FL在医学领域的临床应用提供了全面而深入的评价框架。

</details>


### [32] [LUMI: Tokenizer-Agnostic LLM-Based Lossless Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08221)
*Chris Xing Tian,Chengkai Wu,Ziyu Wang,Rongqun Lin,Kecheng Chen,Xiandong Meng,Haoliang Li,Shiqi Wang,Siwei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LUMI提出了一种无分词器依赖的框架，将冻结的大语言模型用于无损RGB图像压缩，通过像素嵌入模块和位置编码替代传统的像素转文本方法，在多个骨干模型和图像基准上取得了有竞争力的压缩率与跨域鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于LLM的无损图像压缩方法依赖分词器将像素值转为文本token，导致压缩过程与分词器行为、词汇表数字token及模型家族特定适配相耦合，缺乏通用性和统一接口。

Method: LUMI使用像素嵌入模块将原始像素强度与通道信息映射到LLM的连续嵌入空间，引入块内位置编码保留二维空间结构，并通过256路预测头输出原生像素字母表上的概率分布。仅训练像素嵌入、位置编码、软前缀参数和预测头，LLM骨干保持冻结。

Result: 在自然图像、医学图像和遥感图像基准上，以LLaMA、Qwen和Gemma为骨干，LUMI提供了跨分词器家族的统一接口，取得了有竞争力的压缩率，并在跨域鲁棒性上优于基于分词器的LLM压缩基线。

Conclusion: LLM-based无损图像压缩可以建模为冻结基础模型在像素空间上的适配问题，而非依赖分词器的语言符号建模。

Original Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based lossless image compression methods typically represent pixel data through the native text interface of a pretrained model, converting pixel values into token sequences that the LLM processes through its vocabulary head. This design shows that pretrained language models can provide probability estimates for image coding, but it also couples compression to tokenizer behavior, vocabulary-specific numeric tokens, and model-family-specific adaptation. In this paper, we present LUMI (LLM-based Unified Model-agnostic lossless Image compression), a tokenizer-agnostic framework for lossless RGB image compression with frozen LLM backbones. LUMI replaces pixel-as-text tokenization with a pixel embedding module that maps raw intensity and channel information into the continuous embedding space of the LLM. It further introduces intra-patch position encoding to retain two-dimensional spatial structure after flattening, and uses a 256-way prediction head to produce probabilities over the native pixel alphabet. Only the pixel embedding, position encoding, soft-prefix parameters, and prediction head are trained, while the LLM backbone remains fixed. Experiments on natural, medical, and remote-sensing image benchmarks with LLaMA, Qwen, and Gemma backbones show that LUMI provides a unified interface across tokenizer families, achieves competitive compression rates, and improves cross-domain robustness over tokenizer-based LLM compression baselines. These results formulate LLM-based lossless image compression as pixel-space adaptation of frozen foundation models rather than tokenizer-specific language-symbol modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的无损图像压缩方法通常通过预训练模型的原生文本接口来表示像素数据，将像素值转换为LLM通过其词表头处理的token序列。这种设计表明，预训练语言模型可以为图像编码提供概率估计，但同时也将压缩过程与分词器行为、词表特定的数字token以及模型家族特定的适配相耦合。在本文中，我们提出了LUMI（LLM-based Unified Model-agnostic lossless Image compression），一种无分词器依赖的框架，用于在冻结LLM骨干网络下进行无损RGB图像压缩。LUMI用像素嵌入模块替代了将像素视为文本的分词方式，该模块将原始强度与通道信息映射到LLM的连续嵌入空间中。它还引入了块内位置编码以在展平后保留二维空间结构，并使用256路预测头输出原生像素字母表上的概率分布。仅像素嵌入、位置编码、软前缀参数和预测头被训练，而LLM骨干网络保持冻结。在自然图像、医学图像和遥感图像基准上，以LLaMA、Qwen和Gemma为骨干网络的实验表明，LUMI提供了跨分词器家族的统一接口，取得了有竞争力的压缩率，并在跨域鲁棒性上优于基于分词器的LLM压缩基线。这些结果将基于LLM的无损图像压缩表述为冻结基础模型在像素空间上的适配，而非针对分词器的语言符号建模。

</details>


### [33] [Multimodal 3D LUT Generation via StatLUT with Statistical Features for Photorealistic Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08227)
*Yifan Wang,Zhixiang Hao,Yu Wang,Congchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StatLUT提出了一种多模态3D LUT生成框架，通过Lab色彩空间统计特征提取器解耦颜色与结构语义，利用Transformer序列生成模型预测拓扑平滑的3D LUT，并引入轻量级扩散Transformer实现文本驱动的颜色风格迁移，在真实感风格迁移任务上显著超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的照片级风格迁移方法存在三个瓶颈：(1) 预训练图像编码器导致语义纠缠，产生不自然的空间扭曲；(2) 像素级映射范式忽略色域拓扑结构，导致颜色带状伪影；(3) 缺乏直观的文本驱动多模态控制能力。StatLUT旨在同时解决这三个问题。

Method: (1) 提出Lab-Extractor，在Lab色彩空间提取空间无关的统计特征，从根本上解耦颜色分布与结构语义；(2) 将3D LUT生成建模为基于Transformer的Seq2Seq翻译任务，使用多维残差映射器(MR-Mapper)预测拓扑平滑的3D LUT；(3) 提出H-Diffuser，一种轻量级扩散Transformer，直接从自然语言提示合成统计特征，实现文本驱动的颜色分级。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上的大量实验表明，StatLUT在视觉质量和定量指标上均显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: StatLUT开创了一种高度鲁棒且灵活的多模态照片级风格迁移范式，有效解决了语义纠缠、色域拓扑失真和单模态限制等关键问题。

Original Abstract: Photorealistic Style Transfer (PST) aims to transfer the color and tonal style of a reference to a content image while strictly preserving its structural integrity. However, existing deep learning-based methods inherently suffer from semantic entanglement caused by pre-trained image encoders, leading to unnatural spatial distortions. Moreover, current pixel-level mapping paradigms often ignore color gamut topology, resulting in color banding, while also lacking the multimodal capability for intuitive text-driven control. To address these bottlenecks, we propose StatLUT, an innovative multimodal framework for 3D LUT generation. First, we bypass traditional encoders and introduce a Lab-Extractor to derive spatially-agnostic statistical features, fundamentally decoupling color distributions from structural semantics to ensure artifact-free rendering. Second, we formulate LUT generation as a Transformer-based Seq2Seq translation task, utilizing a Multi-dimensional Residual Mapper (MR-Mapper) to predict topologically smooth 3D LUTs. Finally, to break the single-modal barrier, we propose the H-Diffuser, a lightweight Diffusion Transformer that directly synthesizes statistical features from natural language prompts, enabling flexible text-driven color grading. Extensive experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that StatLUT significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both visual quality and quantitative metrics, pioneering a highly robust and flexible paradigm for multimodal photorealistic style transfer.

中文摘要翻译: 照片级风格迁移旨在将参考图像的色彩和色调风格迁移到内容图像上，同时严格保持其结构完整性。然而，现有基于深度学习的方法由于预训练图像编码器的固有缺陷，不可避免地遭受语义纠缠问题，导致不自然的空间扭曲。此外，当前的像素级映射范式常常忽略色域拓扑结构，产生颜色带状伪影，同时缺乏直观的文本驱动控制的多模态能力。为应对这些瓶颈，我们提出StatLUT，一种创新的多模态3D LUT生成框架。首先，我们绕过传统编码器，引入Lab-Extractor提取空间无关的统计特征，从根本上解耦颜色分布与结构语义，确保无伪影渲染。其次，我们将LUT生成建模为基于Transformer的序列到序列翻译任务，利用多维残差映射器预测拓扑平滑的3D LUT。最后，为突破单模态屏障，我们提出H-Diffuser，一种轻量级扩散Transformer，可直接从自然语言提示合成统计特征，实现灵活的文本驱动颜色分级。在标准基准上的大量实验表明，StatLUT在视觉质量和定量指标上均显著优于最先进方法，开创了多模态照片级风格迁移中高度鲁棒且灵活的新范式。

</details>


### [34] [TVTA: Trajectory-Aware Viseme-Guided Temporal Aggregation for Event-Based Lip Reading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08236)
*Jingrong Zheng,Hongwei Ren,Xiangqian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个基于事件相机的时序增强唇语识别框架，包含轨迹感知差分聚合（TDA）、视素引导聚合（VGA）和EMA教师-学生训练策略，解决现有方法空间压缩过早、时序建模不足的问题，在DVS-Lip基准上验证了有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有事件相机唇语识别方法通常在充分的时序建模之前进行空间压缩，这可能会抑制区分相似唇部运动所需的稀疏、局部运动轨迹；此外，大多方法仅在词汇分类层面优化时序表征，对底层发音结构的约束较弱。

Method: 1) 轨迹感知差分聚合（TDA）：在空间自适应聚合之前，对每个空间位置进行局部时序建模；2) 视素引导聚合（VGA）：由CTC解码器和视素引导门控聚合分支组成的统一时序模块，注入视素感知的序列监督并改进最终时序聚合；3) EMA教师-学生训练策略：增强模型在强事件扰动下的鲁棒性。

Result: 在DVS-Lip基准上的实验验证了所提设计的有效性；大量消融研究进一步验证了TDA、VGA和教师-学生一致性策略的贡献。定性解码结果也表明，所提基于CTC的时序建模能从事件流中学习有意义的视素感知结构。

Conclusion: 提出的时序增强框架有效解决了现有时序建模不足的问题，通过TDA、VGA和EMA教师-学生训练策略，显著提升了事件相机唇语识别的性能，并能够从事件流中学习到有意义的视素结构信息。

Original Abstract: Event-based lip reading has recently emerged as a promising direction for visual speech recognition, benefiting from the high temporal resolution and motion sensitivity of event cameras. However, existing methods typically perform spatial compression before sufficient temporal modeling, which may suppress sparse and localized motion trajectories that are crucial for distinguishing similar lip movements. Moreover, most current approaches optimize temporal representations mainly at the word-classification level, leaving the underlying articulatory structure weakly constrained. To address these limitations, we propose a temporally enhanced framework for event-based lip reading. First, we introduce Trajectory-Aware Differential Aggregation (TDA), which performs local temporal modeling at each spatial location before adaptive spatial aggregation. Second, we propose Viseme-Guided Aggregation (VGA), a unified temporal module composed of a CTC decoder and a viseme-guided gated aggregation branch, which injects viseme-aware sequence supervision and improves final temporal aggregation for word recognition. Third, we incorporate an EMA teacher--student training strategy to enhance robustness under strong event perturbations. Experiments on the DVS-Lip benchmark verify the effectiveness of the proposed design, and extensive ablation studies further validate the contributions of TDA, VGA, and teacher--student consistency. Qualitative decoding results also demonstrate that the proposed CTC-based temporal modeling learns meaningful viseme-aware structure from event streams.

中文摘要翻译: 基于事件的唇语识别最近已成为视觉语音识别的一个有前景的方向，得益于事件相机的高时间分辨率和运动敏感性。然而，现有方法通常在充分的时序建模之前进行空间压缩，这可能会抑制区分相似唇部运动所需的关键稀疏局部运动轨迹。此外，目前大多数方法主要在词汇分类层面优化时序表征，对底层发音结构的约束较弱。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种时序增强的基于事件的唇语识别框架。首先，我们引入了轨迹感知差分聚合（TDA），该方法在自适应空间聚合之前对每个空间位置进行局部时序建模。其次，我们提出了视素引导聚合（VGA），这是一个由CTC解码器和视素引导门控聚合分支组成的统一时序模块，该模块注入视素感知的序列监督并改进用于词汇识别的最终时序聚合。第三，我们引入了一种EMA教师-学生训练策略，以增强模型在强事件扰动下的鲁棒性。在DVS-Lip基准上的实验验证了所提设计的有效性，大量的消融研究进一步验证了TDA、VGA和教师-学生一致性策略的贡献。定性解码结果也表明，所提基于CTC的时序建模能从事件流中学习有意义的视素感知结构。

</details>


### [35] [Closing the Null Space: Guidance-Aware Quantization for Classifier-Free Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08241)
*Abdullah Al Shafi,Sumaiya Rahim Suma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出现有量化方法忽略了CFG扩散模型的条件/无条件双分支结构，导致系统性延迟陷阱和算法级分支漂移陷阱，并提出GAMP方法通过直接校准引导预测、逐层分配位宽来避免分支漂移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有后训练量化方法将CFG扩散模型视为单分支网络，忽略了其固有的条件/无条件配对结构，导致系统层面两遍推理的延迟被忽视，算法层面仅校准引导间隙会导致无条件分支任意漂移，破坏引导预测质量。

Method: 提出引导感知混合精度量化（GAMP），直接在引导预测上进行校准，从引导输出退化中推导逐层激活位宽敏感性，通过贪心背包算法分配位宽，从根本上防止无条件分支漂移。

Result: 从理论上证明了分支漂移陷阱的存在，并通过假阳性实验证实：标准诊断指标下最佳校准模型反而产生最差的样本质量。

Conclusion: GAMP方法能够有效避免无条件分支漂移，实现更可靠的CFG扩散模型量化。

Original Abstract: Deploying classifier-free guidance (CFG) diffusion models under real-world compute budgets requires quantization, yet existing post-training quantization (PTQ) methods treat CFG models as single-branch networks, ignoring the paired conditional/unconditional structure that CFG inference fundamentally relies on. This structural blind spot has two consequences. At the system level, the two-pass CFG execution pattern imposes a latency overhead that parameter-count and bit-operation metrics conceal entirely, and commodity INT8 inference stacks fail to realize the theoretical efficiency gains that BOPs calculations promise. At the algorithmic level, calibrating against the guidance gap alone admits an exact null space: a quantized model can achieve perfect gap-fidelity diagnostics while the unconditional branch drifts arbitrarily, corrupting every guided prediction at inference time. This paper terms this the branch-drift trap, proves its existence analytically, and confirms it empirically through a false-positive result in which the best-calibrated model by standard diagnostics simultaneously produces the worst sample quality. To close the trap, Guidance-Aware Mixed Precision (GAMP) is proposed, which calibrates directly on the guided prediction, derives per-layer activation-bit sensitivity from guided-output degradation, and allocates bits via a greedy knapsack -- provably preventing unconditional branch drift by construction.

中文摘要翻译: 在实际计算预算下部署分类器引导（CFG）扩散模型需要进行量化，然而现有的后训练量化方法将CFG模型视为单分支网络，忽略了CFG推理根本上依赖的条件/无条件配对结构。这一结构盲点带来了两方面后果。在系统层面，两遍执行的CFG模式引入了延迟开销，这完全被参数量和位操作指标所掩盖，且现有的INT8推理堆栈无法实现BOPs计算所承诺的理论效率提升。在算法层面，仅针对引导间隙进行校准会引入一个精确的零空间：量化模型可以在间隙保真度诊断上达到完美，但无条件分支却任意漂移，从而在推理时破坏每一个引导预测。本文将这一现象称为分支漂移陷阱，理论上证明了其存在性，并通过一个假阳性结果在实证上加以确认：标准诊断指标下最佳校准的模型同时产生了最差的样本质量。为消除该陷阱，本文提出了引导感知混合精度量化（GAMP），该方法直接在引导预测上进行校准，从引导输出退化中推导逐层激活位宽敏感性，并通过贪心背包算法分配位宽——从根本上保证防止无条件分支漂移。

</details>


### [36] [SkelGen4D: Weakly-Supervised Skeleton-Based 4D Generation for Text-Driven Mesh Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08246)
*Hao Feng,Zhi Zuo,Jia-Hui Pan,Ka-Hei Hui,Zhengzhe Liu,Dian Zhang,Haoran Xie,Bin Sheng,Jingyu Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SkelGen4D，一种弱监督的前馈框架，通过骨骼驱动的显式控制实现文本到网格动画的4D生成，无需逐帧骨骼标注，在多个基准上达到或超越全监督方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有4D生成方法多基于SDS优化或视频驱动动画，缺乏与标准工业管线兼容的显式控制与编辑能力。本文旨在建立一种骨骼驱动的动画框架，实现精确可控、可编辑且符合工业标准的文本驱动4D网格动画生成。

Method: 首先通过可微拟合从动画网格中恢复时序一致的伪骨骼；然后以前馈方式生成文本条件的骨骼运动序列；最后用Motion-GRPO进行细化，确保时序连贯、物理合理且关节分明的动画。

Result: 在Truebones Zoo和Diffusion4D两个大规模基准上，弱监督的骨骼建模匹配或超越了全监督基线，并能扩展到多样化的物体类别，生成高质量的文本驱动网格动画。

Conclusion: SkelGen4D验证了弱监督骨骼建模在4D生成中的有效性，支持灵活的运动编辑，并与标准动画制作管线兼容，为文本驱动的网格动画提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Original Abstract: We study 4D generation to synthesize temporally coherent sequences of 3D geometry for animation and content creation. In contrast to existing SDS-based optimization methods and video-driven animation approaches, we adopt a skeleton-driven animation framework aligned with standard industrial pipelines, which enables explicit control and editing. To this end, we propose SkelGen4D, a weakly supervised feed-forward framework for text-driven mesh animation that generates explicit skeleton motions without requiring per-frame skeleton annotations. SkelGen4D first recovers temporally consistent pseudo-skeletons from animated meshes via differentiable fitting, and then generates text-conditioned skeleton motion sequences in a feed-forward manner, further refined with Motion-GRPO to ensure temporally coherent, physically plausible, and articulated animation. We evaluate our method on two large-scale benchmarks, Truebones Zoo and Diffusion4D. Our results show that our weakly supervised skeleton modeling matches or surpasses fully supervised baselines while scaling to diverse object categories for high-quality text-driven mesh animation. Further, our method supports flexible motion editing and is aligned with standard animation production pipelines.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究4D生成，以合成用于动画和内容创作的时序连贯的3D几何序列。与现有的基于SDS的优化方法和视频驱动的动画方法不同，我们采用与标准工业管线一致的骨骼驱动动画框架，从而实现显式控制和编辑。为此，我们提出SkelGen4D，一种弱监督的前馈框架，用于文本驱动的网格动画，无需逐帧骨骼标注即可生成显式骨骼运动。SkelGen4D首先通过可微拟合从动画网格中恢复时序一致的伪骨骼，然后以前馈方式生成文本条件的骨骼运动序列，并通过Motion-GRPO进一步细化，确保时序连贯、物理合理且关节分明的动画。我们在两个大规模基准Truebones Zoo和Diffusion4D上评估了我们的方法。结果表明，我们的弱监督骨骼建模匹配或超越了全监督基线，同时能扩展到多样化的物体类别，生成高质量的文本驱动网格动画。此外，我们的方法支持灵活的运动编辑，并与标准动画制作管线兼容。

</details>


### [37] [HSA: Hierarchical Slot Attention for Multi-granularity Scene-Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08249)
*Neelu Madan,Rongzhen Zhao,Andreas Mogelmose,Juho Kannala,Joni Pajarinen,Graham W. Taylor,Thomas B. Moeslund*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化Slot注意力（HSA），通过单一模型从少量监督中学习多粒度的语义场景分解（整体/前景-背景、语义类别、全景实例），在COCO和PASCAL VOC上显著优于平面基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Slot注意力方法将场景分解为单一粒度的平面槽集合，且基于外观而非语义进行分解。然而人类通过语义层次理解场景（前景/背景、物体类别、个体实例），这种语义层次无法在没有监督的情况下自然产生，因为类别名称是人为构造而非视觉模式。

Method: 提出层次化Slot注意力（HSA），在单一模型中同时学习三个层次：整体层（前景/背景）、语义层（物体类别）、全景层（个体实例）。仅需10%标注数据，结合层次化对齐损失联合训练三个层次。引入分组纯度和包含性指标来衡量层次结构是否在表示空间中被编码。

Result: 在COCO数据集上，HSA相比最强平面基线的ARI提升：整体层+41.5，语义层+14.6，全景层+10.4；在PASCAL VOC上获得更大提升；且仅需单一模型而非三个独立模型。

Conclusion: HSA通过层次化语义场景分解有效克服了现有Slot注意力方法的局限性，以少量标注数据实现了多粒度分解，在性能上大幅超越平面基线方法。

Original Abstract: Slot attention is a powerful framework for object-centric learning, decomposing visual scenes into latent slots through iterative competitive attention. However, existing methods share two critical limitations: they decompose scenes into a flat set of slots at a single granularity, and this decomposition is based on appearance rather than semantics. Yet humans understand scenes through semantic hierarchies: separating foreground from background, recognizing object categories, and identifying individual instances. Crucially, such semantic hierarchies cannot emerge without supervision, because category names are human constructs, not visual patterns. We propose Hierarchical Slot Attention (HSA), which learns multi-granularity semantic scene decomposition from a single model. HSA decomposes scenes at three levels: holistic (foreground/background), semantic (object categories), and panoptic (individual instances). Using only 10\% labeled data, combined with hierarchical alignment loss, HSA learns all three levels jointly. We further introduce grouping purity and containment to measure whether the hierarchy is encoded in representation space, not just output masks. Experiments on COCO and PASCAL VOC demonstrate that HSA outperforms the strongest flat baseline by up to \textbf{$+$41.5} ARI at holistic, \textbf{$+$14.6} at semantic, and \textbf{$+$10.4} at panoptic level on COCO, with even larger gains on Pascal VOC, while requiring a single model instead of three. Code will be made available upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: Slot注意力是一种强大的面向对象的场景分解框架，通过迭代竞争注意力将视觉场景分解为潜在槽。然而，现有方法存在两个关键局限性：它们将场景分解为单一粒度的平面槽集合，并且这种分解基于外观而非语义。然而，人类通过语义层次理解场景：分离前景与背景、识别物体类别、以及识别个体实例。关键在于，这种语义层次无法在没有监督的情况下自然产生，因为类别名称是人为构造而非视觉模式。我们提出层次化Slot注意力（HSA），它从单一模型中学习多粒度的语义场景分解。HSA在三个层次上分解场景：整体层（前景/背景）、语义层（物体类别）和全景层（个体实例）。仅使用10%的标注数据，结合层次化对齐损失，HSA联合学习所有三个层次。我们进一步引入分组纯度和包含性指标来衡量层次结构是否在表示空间中编码，而不仅仅在输出掩码中。在COCO和PASCAL VOC上的实验表明，HSA在COCO上相比最强平面基线在整体层ARI提升高达+41.5，语义层+14.6，全景层+10.4，在PASCAL VOC上获得更大增益，同时仅需单一模型而非三个独立模型。代码将在论文接收后公开。

</details>


### [38] [On the Design of Mixture-of-Experts for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08250)
*In-Hwan Jin,Hyeongju Mun,Joonsoo Kim,Kugjin Yun,Kyeongbo Kong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对动态场景重建中单一变形模型鲁棒性不足的问题，从混合专家（MoE）视角提出两种多变形建模策略：MoDE（联合优化多个变形专家，共享规范高斯表示）和MoE-GS（独立优化变形专家，通过独立路由阶段组合），为动态3D高斯表示中的多变形建模提供了两种不同的集成范式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有动态3D高斯泼溅方法通常依赖单一变形模型，难以应对真实世界中异构且空间变化的复杂运动模式，缺乏对多样化动态场景的鲁棒性。

Method: 从混合专家（MoE）视角出发，提出两种多变形建模策略：1）MoDE：将多个变形专家直接集成到可变形高斯泼溅管线中，通过联合优化在共享规范高斯表示上协同工作；2）MoE-GS：变形专家独立优化后，通过独立的路由阶段进行组合，专家交互发生在非规范高斯表示上。

Result: 两种方法提供了多变形建模的替代策略，阐明了不同集成约束如何影响动态3D高斯表示中变形专家的设计与行为。

Conclusion: 多变形建模能有效提升动态场景重建的鲁棒性，而集成约束（联合优化 vs. 独立优化+路由）会显著影响变形专家的交互方式及其在统一表示中的行为表现。

Original Abstract: Dynamic scene reconstruction remains challenging due to the heterogeneous and spatially varying nature of real-world motion. Although recent 3D Gaussian Splatting methods have introduced diverse deformation formulations for dynamic novel view synthesis, each method typically relies on a single deformation model within its representation, which limits robustness across diverse dynamic scenarios. In this work, we study a fundamental problem-multi-deformation modeling for dynamic 3D Gaussian representations-under two distinct integration constraints that differ in when and how multiple deformation experts interact during training. From a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) perspective, we view multi-deformation modeling as the problem of combining multiple specialized deformation models within a unified 3D representation. We first introduce Mixture of Deformation Experts (MoDE), which integrates multiple deformation experts directly into the deformable Gaussian Splatting pipeline through joint optimization. In MoDE, experts operate on a shared canonical Gaussian representation, enabling multi-deformation modeling without introducing additional training stages or modifying the original optimization schedule. In contrast, we further present Mixture of Experts for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting (MoE-GS) under a different integration constraint, where deformation experts are optimized independently and combined through a separate routing stage. As a result, expert interaction occurs over non-canonical Gaussian representations after individual optimization. Together, these two approaches provide alternative strategies for multi-deformation modeling, clarifying how integration constraints shape the design and behavior of deformation experts in dynamic 3D Gaussian representations. Our code is available at: https://github.com/cvsp-lab/MoE-GS-studio.

中文摘要翻译: 动态场景重建由于真实世界运动的异构性和空间变化特性仍然具有挑战性。尽管最近的3D高斯泼溅方法为动态新视角合成引入了多种变形公式，每种方法通常在其表示中依赖单一的变形模型，这限制了其在多样化动态场景中的鲁棒性。在这项工作中，我们研究了一个基础问题——动态3D高斯表示的多变形建模——在两个不同的集成约束下，这两个约束的区别在于多个变形专家在训练过程中何时以及如何交互。从混合专家（MoE）的角度来看，我们将多变形建模视为在统一3D表示中组合多个专门化变形模型的问题。我们首先引入变形专家混合（MoDE），它将多个变形专家直接集成到可变形高斯泼溅管线中，通过联合优化实现。在MoDE中，专家在共享的规范高斯表示上操作，无需引入额外的训练阶段或修改原始优化调度即可实现多变形建模。相比之下，我们进一步提出了动态高斯泼溅的专家混合（MoE-GS），它采用不同的集成约束，其中变形专家被独立优化并通过单独的路由阶段进行组合。因此，专家交互发生在个体优化后的非规范高斯表示上。这两种方法共同为多变形建模提供了替代策略，阐明了集成约束如何塑造动态3D高斯表示中变形专家的设计和行为。我们的代码可在：https://github.com/cvsp-lab/MoE-GS-studio 获取。

</details>


### [39] [UniRef-UAV: A Multimodal Benchmark for Universal Referring in UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08267)
*Haibin Tian,Huichao Xie,Xuelin Qian,Ruitao Lu,Junwei Han,Dingwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出面向无人机航拍场景的通用指代表达理解任务（Universal Referring），并构建多模态基准UniRef-UAV以及轻量级基线模型UAV-URNet，支持文本、图像及图文混合查询下的无目标/单目标/多目标定位，实验表明该方法在判别无目标场景和跨域泛化上优于大型多模态模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有指代表达理解（REC）基准和方法主要围绕纯文本查询和单目标输出构建，无法适用于无人机航拍中涉及参考图像、多模态指令、目标缺失以及多个有效目标实例的实际场景，因此需要扩展查询模态与输出基数以填补这一空白。

Method: 提出UAV-URNet，一种检测风格的基线方法，将异构查询（文本/图像/图文混合）映射到共享查询空间，并通过集合预测（set prediction）输出可变大小的目标集合，实现无目标/单目标/多目标的统一定位。

Result: 实验表明UAV-URNet提供稳定可复现的基线，在无目标判别上更加一致，且比大型通用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）更轻量、更易复现；多模态查询有助于减少视觉查询歧义并促进更统一的查询-目标对齐空间。

Conclusion: 本文通过扩展查询模态和输出基数，系统性地推进了无人机航拍场景下的指代表达理解任务，所构建的基准和基线方法为后续研究提供了可靠基础。

Original Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) increasingly rely on visual grounding capabilities to localize task-relevant targets from diverse instructions in complex aerial scenes. Existing referring expression comprehension (REC) benchmarks and methods, however, are largely built around text-only queries and single-object outputs, which limits their applicability to practical UAV scenarios involving reference images, multimodal instructions, absent targets, and multiple valid target instances. To address this gap, we introduce \emph{Universal Referring}, a generalized UAV referring task that jointly expands the query modality and the output cardinality. We construct \emph{UniRef-UAV}, a multimodal benchmark that supports text-only, image-only, and text+image queries with modality-dependent target cardinality, where text-only and text+image queries admit no-target, single-target, and multi-target grounding while image-only queries focus on existence-aware single-instance grounding. It also provides in-domain and cross-domain evaluation protocols for visual-query generalization. We further present \emph{UAV-URNet}, a detection-style baseline that maps heterogeneous queries into a shared query space and predicts variable-size target sets through set prediction. Extensive experiments show that UAV-URNet provides a stable and reproducible baseline with more consistent no-target discrimination and a more lightweight, reproducible implementation than large general-purpose MLLMs. Additional domain analysis, query-representation analysis, and ablation studies demonstrate that multimodal queries help reduce visual-query ambiguity and promote a more unified query--target alignment space. The annotations, visual query crops/images, train/validation/test splits, evaluation scripts, and baseline code will be made publicly available to facilitate reproducible research.

中文摘要翻译: 无人机（UAV）在执行复杂航拍场景中的任务时，越来越依赖视觉定位能力来根据多样化的指令定位与任务相关的目标。然而，现有的指代表达理解（REC）基准和方法主要围绕纯文本查询和单目标输出构建，这限制了它们在涉及参考图像、多模态指令、目标缺失以及多个有效目标实例的实用无人机场景中的适用性。为弥补这一不足，我们提出了一种广义的无人机指代表达任务——通用指代表达（Universal Referring），该任务同时扩展了查询模态和输出基数。我们构建了UniRef-UAV多模态基准，支持纯文本、纯图像以及文本+图像查询，并具有模态相关的目标基数：其中纯文本查询和文本+图像查询允许无目标、单目标和多目标定位，而纯图像查询则聚焦于存在感知的单实例定位。该基准还提供了用于视觉查询泛化的域内和跨域评估协议。我们进一步提出了UAV-URNet，一种检测风格的基线方法，该方法将异构查询映射到共享查询空间，并通过集合预测输出可变大小的目标集合。大量实验表明，UAV-URNet提供了一个稳定且可复现的基线，在无目标判别上更加一致，且比大型通用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）更轻量、更易复现。额外的域分析、查询表示分析以及消融研究表明，多模态查询有助于减少视觉查询的歧义，并促进更统一的查询-目标对齐空间。为促进可复现研究，本文的标注、视觉查询裁剪图/图像、训练/验证/测试划分、评估脚本以及基线代码将公开发布。

</details>


### [40] [Progression as Latent Drift: Generative Forecasting of Slow-Evolving Pathologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08270)
*Yuxiang Feng,Juncheng Wang,Chao Xu,Wenlong Hou,Huihan Wang,Yijie Qian,Yang Liu,Baigui Sun,Yong Liu,Shujun Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对纵向MRI中缓慢进展的神经退行性疾病预测问题，论文提出Latent Drift框架，通过在压缩语义表征中学习变化而非合成全分辨率解剖图像，避免恒等坍缩和连续插值陷阱两大问题，并结合有限标量量化抑制噪声，在3D脑MRI预测上优于扩散模型和自回归Transformer基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 纵向MRI中真正的进展信号微弱，直接迁移现代生成式序列模型不可靠：训练被稳定的基线解剖结构主导，且受密集的样本特异性噪声干扰。现有模型存在恒等坍缩（优化趋向复制当前解剖结构，无法学习微弱时序变化）和连续插值陷阱（标准平滑网络无法区分局部生物漂移与广泛噪声，导致虚假变化扩散）两大问题。

Method: 提出Latent Drift渐进式生成框架：1）在压缩语义表征空间中学习变化，而非直接合成全分辨率解剖图像，从而移除像素级恒等预测目标，集中模型容量于与进展相关的动态；2）对学习到的变化表征应用有限标量量化（Finite Scalar Quantization），抑制微小的高频噪声波动，同时保留一致的结构性漂移。

Result: 在纵向3D脑MRI上的实验表明，Latent Drift在生成保真度和临床相关评估指标上均优于扩散模型和自回归Transformer基线，提升了患者特异性神经预测性能。

Conclusion: 在低信噪比场景下，通过解耦身份特征与变化信号，并在离散化的语义空间中学习进展动态，能有效提升神经退行性疾病影像预测的可靠性和准确性。

Original Abstract: Forecasting the future anatomy of slow-evolving neurodegenerative diseases could enable earlier, more targeted intervention and improve clinical trial design, but it remains challenging because true progression signals are subtle in longitudinal MRI. In this low-signal regime, transferring modern generative sequence models directly is unreliable: training is dominated by stable baseline anatomy and confounded by dense, sample-specific nuisance variation. We first provide a theoretical analysis that explains these failures through two modes. Identity collapse occurs when optimization is driven toward reproducing the current anatomy, which prevents the model from learning faint temporal change. The continuous interpolation trap arises when standard smooth networks cannot separate localized biological drift from pervasive noise, which leads to spurious changes that diffuse across the volume. To address both issues, we propose Latent Drift, a progressive generative framework that learns change in a compressed semantic representation rather than synthesizing full-resolution anatomy. This design removes pixel-level identity from the prediction target and concentrates model capacity on progression-relevant dynamics. We further apply Finite Scalar Quantization to the learned change representation, which suppresses small, high-frequency nuisance fluctuations while preserving consistent structural drift. Experiments on longitudinal 3D brain MRI show that Latent Drift improves patient-specific neuro-forecasting over diffusion and autoregressive transformer baselines across generative fidelity and clinically relevant evaluation metrics. Project page: \href{https://cutepkq.github.io/latent-drift}{https://cutepkq.github.io/latent-drift}.

中文摘要翻译: 预测缓慢进展的神经退行性疾病的未来解剖结构，有助于实现更早期、更有针对性的干预并改进临床试验设计，但由于纵向MRI中真正的进展信号极其微弱，这一任务仍具挑战性。在此低信噪比场景下，直接迁移现代生成式序列模型并不可靠：训练过程被稳定的基线解剖结构主导，并受到密集的样本特异性噪声干扰。我们首先通过理论分析揭示了两种失败模式：恒等坍缩指优化过程趋向于复现当前解剖结构，导致模型无法学习微弱的时序变化；连续插值陷阱指标准平滑网络无法区分局部生物漂移与广泛存在的噪声，从而产生弥散于全容积的虚假变化。为解决这两个问题，我们提出了Latent Drift——一种渐进式生成框架，它在压缩的语义表征空间中学习变化，而非合成全分辨率解剖图像。该设计将像素级恒等信息从预测目标中移除，使模型容量集中于与进展相关的动态变化。我们进一步对学习到的变化表征应用有限标量量化，以抑制微小的高频噪声波动，同时保留一致的结构性漂移。在纵向3D脑MRI上的实验表明，Latent Drift在生成保真度和临床相关评估指标上均优于扩散模型和自回归Transformer基线，提升了患者特异性神经预测性能。项目页面：\href{https://cutepkq.github.io/latent-drift}{https://cutepkq.github.io/latent-drift}。

</details>


### [41] [Enhancing the KidSat Model: Integrating Geographical Encoding and Data Quality Assessment for Childhood Poverty Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08281)
*Hou Hin Ip,Ka Nam Lam,Joshua Man Yu Ng,Makkunda Sharma,Seth Flaxman,Codie Gerlach-Wood,H Juliette T Unwin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在KidSat框架基础上，通过改进数据预处理、图像质量筛查和融合球谐函数地理编码，将卫星影像贫困预测的MAE从0.2167降至0.1759（降幅18.83%），证明融合视觉-地理表示与梯度提升树头的组合能显著提升预测精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 卫星影像精准贫困制图面临三大挑战：调查数据标签稀疏且含噪声、图像质量（云层遮挡、损坏）问题、纯图像模型缺乏显式空间结构。现有方法未能充分解决这些问题。

Method: (i) 通过DHS重新聚合，将one-hot维度从103降至51，解决高基数稀疏问题；(ii) 引入两阶段质量筛查流程，过滤云层遮挡或损坏的观测；(iii) 融合DINOv2视觉嵌入与球谐函数（SH）位置特征，并对比SH+SIREN及梯度提升树头（XGBoost/LightGBM）的效果。

Result: MAE从0.2167降至0.1759（相对降低18.83%）；扩展到33个非洲国家时最优配置MAE达0.1658；SH特征持续优于纯图像基线；SH+SIREN在缺乏精心设计目标时可能表现不佳；梯度提升树头最有效利用视觉-地理融合表示中的非线性交互。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一套仅使用公开数据即可提升卫星影像社会经济预测的可扩展原则性方案，验证了地理编码与梯度提升树在贫困预测中的有效性。

Original Abstract: Accurate poverty mapping using satellite imagery is often hindered by (i) noisy and sparse survey-derived supervision, (ii) image quality issues such as cloud cover and image corruption, and (iii) lack of explicit spatial structure in image-only models. Building on the KidSat framework, we develop an enhanced pipeline that improves predictive accuracy via refined data preprocessing, systematic image quality assessment, and mathematically defined geographic encoding. First, we refine the fine-tuning target matrix by resolving high-cardinality sparsity and reducing one-hot dimensionality from 103 to 51 via DHS re-aggregation. Second, we introduce a simple two-stage quality-screening procedure to filter heavily clouded or corrupted observations. Third, we fuse DINOv2 visual embeddings with Spherical Harmonics (SH) location features. Across extensive experiments, these changes reduce MAE from 0.2167 to 0.1759, corresponding to an 18.83% relative reduction on the cluster-level severe-deprivation proportion scale. When extended from 16 to 33 African countries, the best-performing configuration achieves an overall MAE of 0.1658. We find that SH features consistently improve performance over the image-only backbone, whereas higher-capacity coordinate Multi Layer Perception augmentation (SH+SIREN) can underperform without carefully designed objectives. Finally, gradient-boosted tree heads (XGBoost/LightGBM) most effectively exploit nonlinear interactions in the fused visual-geographic representation. These findings provide a scalable and principled recipe for improving satellite-based socioeconomic predictions using only publicly accessible data.

中文摘要翻译: 利用卫星影像进行精准贫困制图常常面临以下障碍：（i）基于调查数据的监督信号稀疏且含噪声，（ii）云层遮挡和图像损坏等影像质量问题，以及（iii）纯图像模型缺乏显式空间结构。基于KidSat框架，我们开发了一条增强管线，通过精细化的数据预处理、系统性的图像质量评估以及数学定义的地理编码来提升预测精度。首先，我们通过解决高基数稀疏性问题，并经由DHS重新聚合将one-hot维度从103降至51，从而优化了微调目标矩阵。其次，我们引入了一个简单的两阶段质量筛查流程，以滤除严重云层遮挡或损坏的观测样本。第三，我们将DINOv2视觉嵌入与球谐函数（SH）位置特征相融合。通过大量实验，这些改进将平均绝对误差（MAE）从0.2167降至0.1759，在聚类级别的严重贫困比例指标上实现了18.83%的相对降幅。当从16个国家扩展至33个非洲国家时，最佳配置的整体MAE达到0.1658。我们发现，SH特征持续优于纯图像骨干网络的表现，而高容量的坐标多层感知机增强（SH+SIREN）在缺乏精心设计的目标函数时可能表现不佳。最后，梯度提升树头（XGBoost/LightGBM）能够最有效地利用融合后的视觉-地理表示中的非线性交互。这些发现为仅使用公开数据提升基于卫星的社会经济预测提供了一套可扩展且原则性的方案。

</details>


### [42] [ARGUS: Accelerated, Robust, General, and Unsupervised Cell Tracking Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08297)
*Noah Jaitner,Kandice Tanner,Ingolf Sack,Hossein S. Aghamiry*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ARGUS是一个无监督、无需GPU的模块化细胞追踪框架，通过自适应检测、光流预测和轨迹优化，在公开数据集上实现了0.897-0.964的追踪精度，运行时间仅为数秒至一分钟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有细胞自动追踪方法在噪声、形态变化、细胞重叠及分裂融合等动态场景下表现不佳，且多依赖训练数据和GPU资源，缺乏通用性和可解释性，亟需一种快速、鲁棒、通用且无监督的解决方案。

Method: ARGUS框架包含四个核心步骤：自适应细胞检测、密集Farneback光流预测、帧间线性分配，以及序列级轨迹片段优化（利用短时隙重新连接断裂轨迹）。

Result: 在公共Cell Tracking Challenge数据集上，检测精度达0.905-0.971，追踪精度达0.897-0.964；运行时间在1分钟以内（处理3帧仅需5-6秒）。

Conclusion: ARGUS是一个模块化、可解释的框架，无需训练数据和GPU基础设施即可适应不同成像模态和生物学应用，代码已开源。

Original Abstract: Background and Objective: Quantitative analysis of cell dynamics is central to modern biological research, providing critical insights into immune cell interactions, disease progression, and drug mechanisms. Automated cell tracking in time-lapse microscopy remains challenging due to noise, morphological variations, overlapping cells, and dynamic events such as divisions and fusions.
  Methods: We present ARGUS, a framework for Accelerated, Robust, General, and Unsupervised Cell Tracking Solutions. ARGUS combines adaptive cell detection, dense Farneback optical-flow prediction, frame-to-frame linear assignment, and a sequence-level tracklet-refinement step that reconnects trajectory fragments across short temporal gaps.
  Results: On publicly available Cell Tracking Challenge datasets, ARGUS achieved detection accuracy of 0.905-0.971 and tracking accuracy of 0.897-0.964, with runtimes within 1 minute (5-6 seconds for 3 frames).
  Conclusions: ARGUS is a modular, interpretable framework that can be adapted to different imaging modalities and biological applications without training data or GPU infrastructure. The implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/Gitinc/argus

中文摘要翻译: 背景与目标：细胞动力学的定量分析是现代生物学研究的核心，为免疫细胞相互作用、疾病进展和药物机制提供了关键见解。由于噪声、形态变化、细胞重叠以及分裂和融合等动态事件，延时显微镜中的自动化细胞追踪仍然具有挑战性。方法：我们提出了ARGUS，一个加速、鲁棒、通用且无监督的细胞追踪解决方案框架。ARGUS结合了自适应细胞检测、密集Farneback光流预测、帧间线性分配以及一个序列级轨迹片段优化步骤，该步骤可重新连接短时间间隔内的断裂轨迹片段。结果：在公开的Cell Tracking Challenge数据集上，ARGUS实现了0.905-0.971的检测精度和0.897-0.964的追踪精度，运行时间在1分钟以内（处理3帧仅需5-6秒）。结论：ARGUS是一个模块化、可解释的框架，无需训练数据或GPU基础设施即可适应不同的成像模态和生物学应用。该实现已在https://github.com/Gitinc/argus公开提供。

</details>


### [43] [Texture Representations in Deep Vision Models: Comparing CNNs, Vision Transformers, and Human Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08321)
*Ludovica de Paolis,Marco Baroni,Alessandro Laio,Eugenio Piasini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究比较了CNN和Vision Transformer（ViT）在纹理感知任务中与人类视觉系统的一致性，发现ViT比CNN更能预测人类的纹理识别表现，且纹理表示主要受网络架构驱动。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究主要关注CNN在物体识别范式下与生物视觉的对齐程度，但视觉系统还包括纹理感知等非物体识别任务。本研究旨在探究在这一更广泛的视觉任务中，CNN和ViT等计算模型与人类视觉系统的一致性如何，以及不同架构之间的差异。

Method: 使用三种不同算法从相同源图像生成不同复杂度的纹理刺激；采用基于秩的统计量量化CNN和三种ViT内部表示中编码的信息；将模型表示与人类心理物理学实验中推断的表示进行相似度比较。

Result: 不同ViT之间的纹理表示具有一致性，但ViT与CNN之间不一致；ViT对不同复杂度的纹理形成相似的表示；与CNN相比，ViT表示能更好地预测人类的纹理识别表现。

Conclusion: ViT可能比CNN更忠实地捕捉了人类视觉系统处理纹理模式的方式；计算模型中纹理刺激的表示主要由网络架构驱动。

Original Abstract: In computational vision science, Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) have emerged as a popular model of biological vision because of the alignment they can exhibit with neural and behavioral data in humans and animals. However, it remains unclear to what extent this alignment persists for visual tasks that extend beyond the canonical object recognition paradigm based on well defined semantic content. In this study, we diverge from the common object-centric view by focusing on another aspect of vision: texture perception. We consider textures of different complexity generated with three different algorithms from the same source images. Using a rank-based statistic, we quantify the information encoded in the internal representations of a CNN and three Vision Transformers (ViTs), and we compare the similarity of these representations to those inferred from human psychophysics data. We find that the representation of textures is aligned in different ViTs, but not between the ViTs and the CNN; that ViTs form similar representations for textures of different complexity; that human performance in recognizing textures can be better predicted from ViTs representations rather than CNN representations. Taken together, these results suggest that ViTs may capture more faithfully than CNNs how texture patterns are visually processed by humans, and that the representations of texture stimuli in computational models may be driven by the network architecture.

中文摘要翻译: 在计算视觉科学中，卷积神经网络（CNN）因其与人类和动物的神经及行为数据所能展现的对齐程度，已成为生物视觉的一种流行模型。然而，对于超出基于明确语义内容的经典物体识别范式的视觉任务，这种对齐程度在多大程度上仍然存在尚不明确。在本研究中，我们偏离了常见的以物体为中心的观点，转而关注视觉的另一个方面：纹理感知。我们考虑了使用三种不同算法从相同源图像生成的具有不同复杂度的纹理。利用一种基于秩的统计量，我们量化了编码在CNN和三种视觉Transformer（ViT）内部表示中的信息，并将这些表示与从人类心理物理学数据中推断出的表示进行相似度比较。我们发现，不同ViT之间的纹理表示具有对齐性，但ViT与CNN之间则不然；ViT对不同复杂度的纹理形成了相似的表示；与CNN表示相比，人类的纹理识别表现可以更好地从ViT表示中预测。综合来看，这些结果表明，ViT可能比CNN更忠实地捕捉了人类视觉系统对纹理模式的处理方式，并且计算模型中纹理刺激的表示可能受网络架构的驱动。

</details>


### [44] [WCog-VLA: A Dual-Level World-Cognitive Vision-Language-Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08375)
*Xuerun Yan,Zhexi Lian,Nuoheng Zhang,Shiyu Fang,Haoran Wang,Chen Lv,Jia Hu,Binyang Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WCog-VLA 提出了一种双层级世界认知 VLA 框架，通过语义级的世界预测与生成级的世界演化实现主动式自动驾驶，在 NAVSIM 基准上达到 SOTA 的 92.9 PDMS 分数。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型要么缺乏全面的世界认知，要么世界预见能力碎片化，本质上局限于反应式驾驶。为解决此问题，论文希望实现主动式自动驾驶。

Method: 提出 WCog-VLA 双层级框架：(1) 语义层：融合 3D 空间感知和智能体注入令牌捕获世界动态，并实现博弈论思维链推理 (Game-CoT)；(2) 生成层：提出对齐解耦扩散 Transformer (ADDT) 作为生成式世界模型，通过场景表示对齐减少去噪步数以加速推理。此外构建了包含 85k Game-CoT 标注的大规模数据集。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 基准上，WCog-VLA 取得了 92.9 的 PDMS 分数，达到当前最优 (SOTA)。

Conclusion: 双层级世界认知框架能够有效桥接语义世界预测与生成世界演化，实现从反应式驾驶到主动式驾驶的跨越。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have advanced end-to-end autonomous driving. However, existing methods either lack comprehensive world cognition or suffer from fragmented world foresight, inherently confining these models to reactive driving. To address this limitation, we propose WCog-VLA, a novel dual-level World-Cognitive VLA framework that successfully bridges semantic world forecasting with generative world evolution to achieve proactive autonomous driving. At the semantic level, WCog-VLA unifies world cognition and reasoning by incorporating 3D spatial perception and injecting agent tokens to capture the world dynamics, while concurrently enabling Game-theoretic Chain-of-Thought (Game-CoT) reasoning. At the generative level, we introduce the Aligned Decoupled Diffusion Transformer (ADDT) as a powerful generative world model that synthesizes physically-plausible joint multi-agent trajectories. Through scene representation alignment, ADDT reduces the number of denoising steps required and thus significantly accelerates inference. To facilitate strategic reasoning, we further construct a large-scale dataset featuring 85k Game-CoT annotations. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark demonstrate that WCog-VLA achieves a State-Of-The-Art (SOTA) PDMS score of 92.9.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型推动了端到端自动驾驶的发展。然而，现有方法要么缺乏全面的世界认知，要么世界预见能力碎片化，本质上将这些模型局限在反应式驾驶中。为解决这一局限，我们提出 WCog-VLA，一种新颖的双层级世界认知 VLA 框架，成功桥接了语义世界预测与生成世界演化，从而实现主动式自动驾驶。在语义层面，WCog-VLA 通过融合 3D 空间感知和注入智能体令牌来捕获世界动态，同时实现博弈论思维链（Game-CoT）推理，从而统一了世界认知与推理。在生成层面，我们引入对齐解耦扩散 Transformer（ADDT）作为强大的生成式世界模型，用于合成物理上合理的多智能体联合轨迹。通过场景表示对齐，ADDT 减少了所需的去噪步数，从而显著加速推理。为促进策略性推理，我们进一步构建了一个包含 85k 条 Game-CoT 标注的大规模数据集。在 NAVSIM 基准上的大量实验表明，WCog-VLA 取得了 92.9 的当前最优（SOTA）PDMS 分数。

</details>


### [45] [Attribute Retrieving for Open-Vocabulary Endoscopic Compositional Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08397)
*Shun Liu,Nan Xi,Yang Liu,Tianyu Luan,Xuan Gong,David Doermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对内窥镜图像中引用分割（RIS）任务面临的高质量标注稀缺和领域特异性问题，构建了大规模基准数据集ReferEndoscopy，并提出基于属性检索的开放词汇内窥镜组合式引用分割框架AR-ERIS，在模拟和真实内窥镜数据上均取得了最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在内窥镜场景中难以捕捉细粒度文本线索，导致性能欠佳且泛化能力有限；同时内窥镜领域缺乏高质量的大规模RIS标注数据。

Method: 构建大规模基准数据集ReferEndoscopy；提出AR-ERIS框架，利用属性检索（attribute retrieval）实现开放词汇的内窥镜组合式引用分割（compositional referring segmentation）。

Result: AR-ERIS在模拟和真实内窥镜数据上均取得了最先进的性能，并展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: AR-ERIS框架结合ReferEndoscopy数据集有效解决了内窥镜RIS任务中的标注稀缺和跨域对齐问题，为内窥镜领域的细粒度视觉理解提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Referring Image Segmentation (RIS) aims to segment image regions specified by natural language, enabling fine-grained and controllable visual understanding. Extending RIS to endoscopic imagery, however, presents unique challenges, including scarce high-quality annotations and complex, domain-specific image-text relationships. Although recent vision-language models demonstrate strong cross-domain alignment, they often fail to capture fine-grained textual cues in endoscopic settings, resulting in suboptimal performance and limited generalization. To address these challenges, we introduce ReferEndoscopy, a large-scale benchmark for RIS in the endoscopy field. Building on this dataset, we propose the Attribute Retrieval-based Endoscopic-RIS (AR-ERIS) framework for open-vocabulary endoscopic compositional referring segmentation. AR-ERIS leverages attribute retrieval for open-vocabulary endoscopic compositional referring segmentation and is pretrained on the curated ReferEndoscopy dataset, achieving state-of-the-art performance with strong generalization across both simulated and real-world endoscopic data. The dataset and code will be publicly released upon completion of the review process.

中文摘要翻译: 引用图像分割（RIS）旨在分割自然语言所指代的图像区域，实现细粒度且可控的视觉理解。将RIS扩展到内窥镜图像带来了独特挑战，包括高质量标注稀缺以及复杂的领域特异性图像-文本关系。尽管最近的视觉-语言模型展现了强大的跨域对齐能力，但它们在内窥镜场景中往往难以捕捉细粒度的文本线索，导致性能欠佳且泛化能力有限。为了解决这些挑战，我们引入了ReferEndoscopy，一个面向内窥镜领域RIS的大规模基准数据集。在该数据集的基础上，我们提出了基于属性检索的内窥镜RIS（AR-ERIS）框架，用于开放词汇的内窥镜组合式引用分割。AR-ERIS利用属性检索实现开放词汇的内窥镜组合式引用分割，并在所整理的ReferEndoscopy数据集上进行预训练，在模拟和真实内窥镜数据上均取得了最先进的性能，展现出强大的泛化能力。数据集和代码将在审稿流程结束后公开发布。

</details>


### [46] [Swapping Faces, Saving Features: A Dual-Purpose Pipeline for Pedestrian Privacy in ITS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08402)
*Roba H. Farouk,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个五阶段人脸替换流水线，在保护自动驾驶数据集中行人隐私的同时保留面部关键属性，以平衡隐私保护与数据可用性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自动驾驶系统需要大量行人图像数据来训练行人意图和轨迹预测模型，但这些数据存在身份盗窃和行人追踪等安全风险。现有隐私保护方法虽能保护隐私，但降低了图像可用性，影响模型效果。因此需要在隐私保护和数据可用性之间取得平衡。

Method: 设计一个五阶段流水线，通过人脸替换技术保护行人隐私，同时保留关键面部属性。该流水线针对Egy-DRiVeS数据集定制，并评估了Roop和Ghost-v2两种人脸替换模型。

Result: Roop模型在多个方面优于Ghost-v2模型，因此被选为该流水线中的人脸替换模型，能够在通过身份隐藏保护行人隐私和通过面部属性保留保证数据可用性之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: Roop人脸替换模型能够有效平衡行人隐私保护与数据可用性，适合用于自动驾驶数据集中的隐私保护处理。

Original Abstract: Large-scale and diverse datasets are needed to train AI models to take real-time decisions for autonomous vehicles (AVs), an intelligent transportation system (ITS) application. Pedestrian intention and trajectory prediction are critical models used in AVs, requiring datasets involving diverse pedestrian images. Unrestricted access to these datasets imposes serious security risks, like identity theft and pedestrian tracking. The challenge is to apply privacy preservation procedures while maintaining the image attributes needed to train the models. Existing privacy methods may preserve the pedestrian's privacy, but degrade the image usability, which hinders the models' effectiveness. This work's focus is to implement a five-stage pipeline to protect pedestrians' privacy through face swapping while keeping the essential facial attributes intact. It should be tailored to satisfy the privacy needs of the Egy-DRiVeS dataset. Moreover, Roop and Ghost-v2 face-swapping models are evaluated. Provenly, Roop outperforms Ghost-v2 in various aspects, as will be discussed. Consequently, Roop is the face-swapping model to be used in the pipeline to strike the balance between pedestrian privacy via identity concealment and data usability via facial attribute preservation.

中文摘要翻译: 训练AI模型为自主车辆（AVs）这一智能交通系统（ITS）应用做出实时决策，需要大规模且多样化的数据集。行人意图和轨迹预测是AVs中使用的关键模型，需要涉及多样化行人图像的数据集。对这些数据集的非受限访问会带来严重的安全风险，如身份盗窃和行人追踪。挑战在于应用隐私保护程序，同时保持训练模型所需的图像属性。现有的隐私方法可能保护了行人的隐私，但降低了图像可用性，从而阻碍了模型的有效性。本工作的重点是实现一个五阶段流水线，通过人脸替换来保护行人的隐私，同时保持关键的面部属性完整。该流水线应针对Egy-DRiVeS数据集的隐私需求量身定制。此外，评估了Roop和Ghost-v2人脸替换模型。事实证明，Roop在多个方面优于Ghost-v2，具体将在文中讨论。因此，Roop是在流水线中使用的人脸替换模型，以在通过身份隐藏实现行人隐私和通过面部属性保留实现数据可用性之间取得平衡。

</details>


### [47] [Track2Map: Online Deformable SLAM with Motion-Aware Pose Optimization in Robotic Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08408)
*Tianyi Song,Sierra Bonilla,Xinwei Ju,Evangelos Mazomenos,Danail Stoyanov,Adam Schmidt,Omid Mohareri,Sophia Bano,Francisco Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Track2Map是一种在线3D高斯溅射管道，能够从手术视频中联合优化相机轨迹和3D可变形场景表示，无需准确的相机轨迹先验，在RAMIS中实现了鲁棒的3D重建，效果优于现有SLAM和非SLAM方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于高斯溅射的可变形3D解剖重建方法多为离线处理，且依赖准确的相机轨迹先验（如机器人运动学数据），当先验缺失或存在噪声时适用性受限。因此需要一种无需先验、在线运行且能联合优化轨迹与场景表示的方法。

Method: 提出Track2Map在线管道：1）利用密集2D点轨迹进行锚定变形初始化，稳定组织运动下的优化过程；2）利用轨迹统计信息检测静态相机时段，解耦相机运动与场景变形；3）在增量建图过程中减少漂移，实现相机轨迹与3D可变形场景表示的联合优化。

Result: 在StereoMIS数据集上，Track2Map在重建质量和相机轨迹精度方面均优于竞争性SLAM方法，同时也优于使用相机轨迹先验的非SLAM方法。

Conclusion: Track2Map有效解决了手术视频中缺乏相机先验时的3D重建问题，通过联合优化轨迹与场景表示，实现了类似SLAM的在线鲁棒重建，为RAMIS中的3D解剖重建提供了新的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Gaussian splatting is the current state-of-the-art for dense, deformable 3D anatomy reconstruction in robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS); however, most pipelines are offline and depend on accurate camera trajectory priors (often from robotic kinematics), limiting applicability when priors are missing or noisy. To address these limitations, we propose Track2Map, an online 3D Gaussian Splatting pipeline that jointly optimizes camera trajectory and 3D deformable scene representation directly from surgical video. Track2Map is therefore capable of robust 3D reconstructions when camera trajectory priors are either absent or noisy, and due to its online nature it effectively works as a Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping (SLAM) method. To stabilize optimization in the presence of tissue motion and ambiguous visual cues, we introduce a track-anchored deformation initialization using dense 2D point tracks. Track statistics are further utilized to disentangle camera motion from scene deformation by detecting static camera periods and reducing drift during incremental mapping. Experiments on StereoMIS show improved reconstruction quality and camera trajectory against competing SLAM methods, as well as compared to non-SLAM methods that utilize camera trajectory priors. The code is available at https://track2map.github.io/.

中文摘要翻译: 高斯溅射是当前机器人辅助微创手术（RAMIS）中密集、可变形3D解剖重建的最先进技术；然而，大多数管道是离线的，且依赖于准确的相机轨迹先验（通常来自机器人运动学），当先验缺失或存在噪声时限制了其适用性。为解决这些限制，我们提出了Track2Map，一种在线3D高斯溅射管道，可直接从手术视频中联合优化相机轨迹和3D可变形场景表示。因此，Track2Map能够在相机轨迹先验缺失或存在噪声时进行鲁棒的3D重建，并且由于其在线特性，它实际上是一种同时定位与地图构建（SLAM）方法。为了在组织运动和模糊视觉线索存在的情况下稳定优化，我们引入了一种基于密集2D点轨迹的锚定变形初始化。进一步利用轨迹统计信息，通过检测静态相机时段并在增量建图过程中减少漂移，来解耦相机运动与场景变形。在StereoMIS上的实验表明，相比竞争性SLAM方法以及利用相机轨迹先验的非SLAM方法，Track2Map在重建质量和相机轨迹方面均有提升。代码可在 https://track2map.github.io/ 获取。

</details>


### [48] [DeltaV: Thinking with Visual State Updates in Unified Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08434)
*Pengjie Wang,Linger Deng,Zujia Zhang,Shaojie Zhang,Zhenbo Luo,Pei Fu,Jian Luan,Xiang Bai,Yuliang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DeltaV提出了一种视觉更新范式，用紧凑的更新令牌替代完整图像生成，通过时序相似性路由器动态分配令牌预算，并结合StructCoT数据集训练，显著减少了视觉令牌冗余并提升了多模态推理性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有统一大规模多模态模型在交错推理中每个视觉状态都生成为完整图像，导致视觉令牌冗余严重，且对稀疏但关键的推理状态转变监督不足。

Method: 提出DeltaV模型，基于历史视觉状态增量预测紧凑的更新令牌来捕获视觉变化；引入时序相似性路由器（TSIM Router），当边际重建增益低于阈值时停止分配令牌；构建包含105万样本、44个任务域的大规模交错多模态推理数据集StructCoT。

Result: 视觉更新范式平均减少55.6%的新生成视觉令牌且不损失重建保真度，多模态推理性能提升3.3%；DeltaV-2B在域内评估上超越更大开源模型8.4%，在外部基准上超越Qwen3-VL-2B达5.9%。

Conclusion: 视觉更新范式能够有效减少冗余、增强对关键状态转变的监督，提升多模态推理效率与性能，且结合大规模数据集训练后可在小规模模型上超越更大模型。

Original Abstract: Current Unified Large Multimodal Models (ULMMs) support interleaved multimodal reasoning through textual reasoning and intermediate visual states, but typically generate each visual state as a full image. This full-image generation paradigm introduces substantial visual-token redundancy and dilutes supervision on sparse yet reasoning-critical state transitions. We propose DeltaV, a ULMM that replaces full-image generation with visual updates. Conditioned on historical visual states, DeltaV incrementally predicts compact update tokens that capture the visual changes across reasoning steps, avoiding repeated modeling of unchanged content. To align the token budget of each update with the magnitude of visual change, DeltaV introduces a temporal similarity (TSIM) Router, which stops allocating tokens once the marginal reconstruction gain falls below a threshold. To support more diverse and generalizable reasoning, we further construct StructCoT, a large-scale interleaved multimodal reasoning dataset with 1.05M samples spanning 44 task domains. Experiments show that the visual-update paradigm reduces newly generated visual tokens by 55.6\% on average without compromising reconstruction fidelity, and improves multimodal reasoning by 3.3\% over full-image generation. Trained with StructCoT and large-scale multimodal data, DeltaV-2B further outperforms substantially larger open-source models by 8.4\% on in-domain multimodal reasoning evaluations and surpasses the comparable-scale Qwen3-VL-2B by 5.9\% on external multimodal reasoning and understanding benchmarks. Code, models, and StructCoT will be released at https://github.com/Pengjie-W/DeltaV.

中文摘要翻译: 当前统一大规模多模态模型（ULMMs）通过文本推理和中间视觉状态支持交错式多模态推理，但通常将每个视觉状态生成为完整图像。这种完整图像生成范式引入了大量视觉令牌冗余，并稀释了对稀疏但推理关键的状态转变的监督。我们提出DeltaV，一种用视觉更新替代完整图像生成的ULMM。基于历史视觉状态，DeltaV增量预测紧凑的更新令牌以捕获推理步骤间的视觉变化，避免对未变化内容的重复建模。为使每个更新的令牌预算与视觉变化幅度对齐，DeltaV引入了时序相似性路由器（TSIM Router），一旦边际重建增益低于阈值即停止分配令牌。为支持更多样化且可泛化的推理，我们进一步构建了StructCoT，一个包含105万样本、覆盖44个任务域的大规模交错多模态推理数据集。实验表明，视觉更新范式在不损失重建保真度的前提下，平均减少55.6%的新生成视觉令牌，并将多模态推理性能提升3.3%。通过StructCoT和大规模多模态数据训练，DeltaV-2B在域内多模态推理评估中进一步超越显著更大的开源模型8.4%，并在外部多模态推理与理解基准上超越同规模的Qwen3-VL-2B达5.9%。代码、模型和StructCoT将在https://github.com/Pengjie-W/DeltaV发布。

</details>


### [49] [Predicting Viticulture Potential through an Ensemble of U-Net and a Geospatial Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08449)
*Jorge Ignacio Perez,Hwaai Kang Kee,Lucas Rassbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合U-Net和地理空间基础模型Prithvi-2.0的集成方法，用于预测法国南部葡萄栽培潜力，在ImageCLEF AI4Agri 2026子任务1中排名第二，准确率达68.32。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统农业潜力评估方法（如调查、实地测量、土壤测试）成本高昂，遥感数据为此提供了更具性价比的替代方案。该研究旨在利用深度学习模型高效预测葡萄栽培潜力，支持可持续土地管理和农业规划。

Method: 采用U-Net与地理空间基础模型Prithvi-2.0的集成方法（ensemble），结合遥感数据进行葡萄栽培潜力预测。

Result: 最佳模型在榜单上取得了68.32的±1准确率，在7支参赛队伍中排名第2。

Conclusion: 该集成方法在葡萄栽培潜力预测任务上表现优异，验证了遥感数据与深度学习模型结合用于农业潜力评估的可行性和有效性。

Original Abstract: Determining agricultural potential is fundamental to sustainable land management and agricultural planning. Remote sensing data is increasingly valuable as an avenue for agricultural potential due to the cost of traditional methods (surveys, in-situ measurements, soil testing, etc). ImageCLEF AI4Agri 2026: Subtask 1 is concerned with the prediction of viticulture potential in Southern France. The DS@GT ARC's submission for Subtask 1 introduces an ensemble of U-Net and a Geospatial Foundation Model (Prithvi-2.0). Our best model achieved a $\pm$1 accuracy of 68.32 on the leaderboard, ranking 2nd among 7 teams. The implementation for this work is publicly available at https://github.com/dsgt-arc/imageclef-ai4agri-2026 .

中文摘要翻译: 确定农业潜力对于可持续土地管理和农业规划至关重要。由于传统方法（调查、实地测量、土壤测试等）成本高昂，遥感数据作为评估农业潜力的途径正变得越来越有价值。ImageCLEF AI4Agri 2026子任务1旨在预测法国南部的葡萄栽培潜力。DS@GT ARC团队在该子任务中提出了一种结合U-Net和地理空间基础模型Prithvi-2.0的集成方法。我们的最佳模型在榜单上取得了68.32的±1准确率，在7支参赛队伍中排名第2。本工作的实现代码已公开发布于https://github.com/dsgt-arc/imageclef-ai4agri-2026。

</details>


### [50] [VEGAS: Human-Aligned Video Caption Evaluation via Gaze](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08489)
*Shenghui Chen,Po-han Li,Ximeng Sun,Shijia Yang,Emad Barsoum,Zicheng Liu,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VEGAS，一种无需训练的、利用测试时眼动数据对视频描述进行个性化评估的指标，通过拒绝采样选择与观众注意力对齐的描述，提升描述与人类关注的匹配度及下游检索性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的视频描述生成方法虽然表现优异，但生成的描述通常无法捕捉单个观众独特的注意力焦点，缺乏个性化。

Method: 提出VEGAS（Video caption Evaluation via GAze Score），一种基于信息论的跨模态指标，利用测试时的眼动追踪数据量化候选描述与观众注意力焦点的匹配程度，通过拒绝采样选择最优描述，无需重新训练模型。

Result: 在自中心活动与教学幻灯片数据集上，VEGAS选出的描述与人类关注的匹配度显著更高，并提升了下游描述到视频的检索性能。

Conclusion: 将观众注意力融入视频描述评估与选择过程具有实际价值，VEGAS作为一种训练自由的指标能够有效实现个性化、关注对齐的描述生成。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models excel at video captioning, yet typically generate descriptions that fail to capture individual viewers' attention. We propose VEGAS (Video caption Evaluation via GAze Score), a training-free metric that leverages test-time gaze to sample personalized, attention-aligned text. It is a cross-modal, information-theoretic metric that quantifies how well a candidate caption matches a viewer's focus. To evaluate VEGAS, we curate a dataset of egocentric activities and instructional slides paired with synchronized gaze and reference annotations. We then select captions based on VEGAS via rejection sampling without model retraining. Experiments show that VEGAS-selected captions align significantly better with human focus and improve downstream caption-to-video retrieval, demonstrating the practical utility of incorporating viewer attention during inference.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型在视频描述生成方面表现优异，但其生成的描述通常无法捕捉单个观众的注意力焦点。我们提出VEGAS（基于注视评分的视频描述评估），一种无需训练的度量指标，利用测试时的眼动数据采样个性化、注意力对齐的文本。该指标是一种跨模态、基于信息论的度量，用于量化候选描述与观众关注焦点的匹配程度。为评估VEGAS，我们构建了一个包含自中心活动与教学幻灯片的数据集，并配以同步的眼动数据与参考标注。随后，我们通过拒绝采样基于VEGAS选择描述，无需重新训练模型。实验表明，VEGAS选出的描述与人类关注的匹配度显著更高，并提升了下游描述到视频的检索性能，证明了在推理过程中融入观众注意力的实际效用。

</details>


### [51] [Cognitive-structured Multimodal Agent for Multimodal Understanding, Generation, and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08497)
*Feng Wang,Canmiao Fu,Zhipeng Huang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种认知结构化的多模态智能体（CMA），通过外部化视觉信息到情景视觉记忆并选择性激活相关片段，解决了长程多模态对话中视觉token爆炸和跨轮次引用不可靠的问题。8B参数的智能体在20轮对话中达到91.4%的检索准确率，超越32B基线模型8.2%，同时推理时间减半。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型将所有历史视觉和文本输入反复喂入共享上下文窗口，导致长程多模态对话中视觉token爆炸和跨轮次引用不可靠，限制了长程对话能力。

Method: 提出认知结构化的多模态智能体（CMA），包含三个核心模块：感知抽象引擎（结构化视觉抽象）、认知检索引擎（跨轮次记忆检索）和多模态执行控制器（自主任务推断与动作规划）。同时设计了统一场景引擎，程序化生成带有细粒度检索标注的结构化多轮对话，并利用强化学习优化抽象和检索策略。

Result: 8B参数的CMA在20轮对话中达到91.4%的检索准确率，超越32B基线模型8.2%，每轮推理时间从23.1秒降至12.7秒（减少近一半）。

Conclusion: 结构化记忆和模块化决策为长程多模态智能体提供了一种比单一参数规模扩展更可扩展、更高效的范式。

Original Abstract: Recent unified multimodal models show a single architecture can jointly perform vision/language understanding and image generation/editing. However, they repeatedly feed all historical visual and textual inputs into a shared context window, limiting long-horizon multimodal dialogue due to visual token explosion and unreliable cross-turn referencing. We propose a Cognitive-structured Multimodal Agent that externalizes visual information into an Episodic Visual Memory and selectively reactivates relevant episodes during reasoning. The agent consists of a Perceptual Abstraction Engine for structured visual abstraction, a Cognitive Retrieval Engine for cross-turn memory retrieval, and a Multimodal Executive Controller for autonomous task inference and action planning. To address the lack of turn-level retrieval supervision in existing datasets, we develop a Unified Scenario Engine that programmatically generates structured multi-turn conversations with fine-grained retrieval annotations, enabling reinforcement learning to optimize abstraction and retrieval policies. We also construct a long-horizon visual-dialogue benchmark stratified by difficulty to evaluate episodic visual recall. Our 8B agent achieves 91.4% retrieval accuracy over 20-turn sessions, surpassing 32B baselines by +8.2% while nearly halving per-turn inference time (23.1s -> 12.7s). We further present the Cognitive-structured Multimodal Agent Harness (CMA-Harness), a tool-augmented deployment of the same cognitive structure integrating persistent multimodal memory, web access, image generation/editing/composition tools, and OpenAI-compatible serving. Structured memory and modular decision-making offer a more scalable, efficient paradigm for long-horizon multimodal agents than monolithic parameter scaling. Code: https://github.com/caseclose/cma-harness ; Project page: https://caseclose.github.io/cma-harness/

中文摘要翻译: 最近的统一多模态模型表明，单一架构可以同时执行视觉/语言理解和图像生成/编辑。然而，它们反复将所有历史视觉和文本输入馈入共享上下文窗口，由于视觉token爆炸和不可靠的跨轮次引用，限制了长程多模态对话。我们提出了一种认知结构化的多模态智能体，将视觉信息外部化到情景视觉记忆中，并在推理过程中选择性激活相关情景。该智能体由用于结构化视觉抽象的感知抽象引擎、用于跨轮次记忆检索的认知检索引擎和用于自主任务推断与动作规划的多模态执行控制器组成。为了解决现有数据集中缺乏轮次级检索监督的问题，我们开发了一个统一场景引擎，程序化地生成带有细粒度检索标注的结构化多轮对话，使强化学习能够优化抽象和检索策略。我们还构建了一个按难度分层的长程视觉对话基准，用于评估情景视觉回忆。我们的8B智能体在20轮会话中达到91.4%的检索准确率，超越32B基线模型8.2%，同时每轮推理时间几乎减半（从23.1秒降至12.7秒）。我们进一步提出了CMA-Harness，这是一个工具增强的部署方案，采用相同的认知结构，集成了持久化多模态记忆、网络访问、图像生成/编辑/合成工具以及与OpenAI兼容的服务接口。结构化记忆和模块化决策为长程多模态智能体提供了一种比单一参数规模扩展更可扩展、更高效的范式。代码：https://github.com/caseclose/cma-harness；项目页面：https://caseclose.github.io/cma-harness/

</details>


### [52] [CT-CLIP Representations for Multimodal Lung Cancer Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08503)
*Sofie Allgöwer,Mikael Johansson,Andreas Hallqvist,Jonas Andersson,Åse Johnsson,Ida Häggström,Jennifer Alvén*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究评估了领域特定的基础模型CT-CLIP在数据受限的临床环境下用于肺癌多模态生存预测的效果，发现冻结的CT-CLIP结合轻量可训练生存头优于临床基线，性能可与甚至超越其他多模态方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度学习驱动的生存模型常受限于缺乏带有可靠结局数据的精选影像队列，亟需探索在数据稀缺的临床场景中如何有效利用预训练基础模型进行生存预测。

Method: 以CT-CLIP基础模型作为特征提取器，对242名肺癌患者的治疗前CT影像和临床变量进行处理，采用冻结编码器、全微调和低秩适配三种适配策略，并结合模态消融实验及与临床和多模态基线方法的对比。

Result: 冻结CT-CLIP模型结合可训练轻量生存头的方法优于临床基线，性能与其他多模态方法相当或更优，并能将患者区分为临床上有意义的高风险和低风险组。

Conclusion: 领域特定的基础模型即使在数据有限的临床场景下，也能作为有效的特征提取器用于多模态生存预测，且简单的冻结策略即可取得令人满意的效果。

Original Abstract: Accurate prognosis prediction is important for treatment planning in lung cancer, but deep learning-driven survival modelling is often limited by the scarcity of curated imaging cohorts with reliable outcome data. This study evaluates whether representations from a domain-specific foundation model can be used for multimodal survival prediction in data-constrained clinical settings. We assess the foundation model CT-CLIP as a feature extractor for pretreatment computed tomography images and clinical variables from 242 diagnosed lung cancer patients. The evaluation includes adaptation strategies based on frozen encoders, full fine-tuning, and low-rank adaptation, together with modality ablations and comparisons with clinical and multimodal baselines. The results show that a frozen CT-CLIP model combined with a trainable lightweight survival head outperforms the clinical baseline and achieves comparable or improved performance relative to other multimodal approaches, and separates patients into clinically meaningful high- and low-risk groups.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的预后预测对于肺癌治疗规划至关重要，但深度学习驱动的生存模型常常受限于缺乏带有可靠结局数据的精选影像队列。本研究评估了领域特定的基础模型所提取的表征能否在数据受限的临床环境中用于多模态生存预测。我们将基础模型CT-CLIP作为特征提取器，对242名确诊肺癌患者的治疗前计算机断层扫描（CT）影像和临床变量进行评估。评估内容包括基于冻结编码器、全微调和低秩适配的适配策略，以及模态消融实验和与临床及多模态基线方法的比较。结果表明，冻结的CT-CLIP模型结合可训练的轻量生存头优于临床基线，性能与其他多模态方法相当或更优，并能将患者区分为临床上有意义的高风险和低风险组。

</details>


### [53] [Do Egocentric Video-Language Models Capture Both Hand- and Object-Centric Cues?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08514)
*Masatoshi Tateno,Alexandros Stergiou,Risa Shinoda,Yoichi Sato,Dima Damen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合手-物遮掩训练和HOI动力学感知解码器的学习范式，以解决视频-语言模型在手-物交互识别中依赖虚假相关性的问题，并构建了DEHOI测试集用于解耦评估，实验表明该方法在多个任务上优于现有模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频-语言模型在识别手-物交互时，常依赖手、物体或环境背景之间的虚假相关性（spurious correlations），而非从手和物体本身的外观与动态进行推理，导致模型泛化能力不足。

Method: (1) 手-物遮掩训练（hand-object masked training）：从部分手或物体观测中实现鲁棒推理；(2) HOI动力学感知解码器（HOI-dynamics-aware decoder）：通过辅助预测手和物体的位置与语义，显式学习以手和物体为中心的嵌入，增强对两类线索的敏感性。

Result: 在DEHOI测试集、标准动作识别、物体状态识别和机器人操作动作识别等多个任务上，该方法均优于现有模型，实现了更鲁棒的HOI理解。

Conclusion: 提出的训练策略能够比现有模型更有效地利用以手和物体为中心的信息，从而在多种HOI相关任务上取得更优性能。

Original Abstract: Hand-object interaction (HOI) recognition requires capturing both hand manipulations and object transformations. However, existing video-language models often fall into shortcuts by relying on spurious correlations among hands, objects, or environmental context, rather than reasoning from the appearance and dynamics of hands and objects themselves. To address this limitation, we propose a new learning paradigm that combines (i) hand-object masked training, which enables robust reasoning from partial hand or object observations, and (ii) an HOI-dynamics-aware decoder that explicitly learns hand- and object-centric embeddings through auxiliary predictions of their locations and semantics, enhancing sensitivity to both cues. To systematically evaluate such cue-specific reasoning, we introduce Cue-Isolated HOI (CI-HOI), a new evaluation that assesses models' ability to predict actions from hand- and object-related cues independently. To enable CI-HOI, we curate the DEHOI testbed, which separates hand- and object-related observations for disentangled HOI evaluation through inpainting. Using DEHOI, we demonstrate both quantitatively and qualitatively that our training strategy exploits hand- and object-centric information more effectively than existing models. Our approach improves over existing models on DEHOI, standard action recognition, object state recognition, and even robot manipulation action recognition, leading to more robust HOI understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 手-物交互（HOI）识别需要同时捕捉手部操作和物体变换。然而，现有的视频-语言模型常常依赖手、物体或环境背景之间的虚假相关性，而非从手和物体本身的外观与动态进行推理，从而陷入捷径学习。为解决这一局限，我们提出了一种新的学习范式，该范式结合了：(i) 手-物遮掩训练，使得模型能够从部分手或物体观测中进行鲁棒推理；(ii) HOI动力学感知解码器，通过辅助预测手和物体的位置与语义来显式学习以手和物体为中心的嵌入，从而增强对两类线索的敏感性。为系统评估这种针对特定线索的推理能力，我们引入了线索隔离HOI（CI-HOI），一种从手和物体相关线索独立评估模型动作预测能力的全新评估方法。为实现CI-HOI，我们构建了DEHOI测试集，通过图像修复将手和物体相关的观测分离，用于解耦的HOI评估。利用DEHOI，我们通过定量和定性实验证明，我们的训练策略比现有模型更有效地利用了以手和物体为中心的信息。我们的方法在DEHOI、标准动作识别、物体状态识别乃至机器人操作动作识别上均优于现有模型，实现了更鲁棒的HOI理解。

</details>


### [54] [Beyond wheelchairs and blindfolds: Investigating disability stereotypes in T2I models with INCLUDE-BENCH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08515)
*Sophia Lichtenberg,Albert Gatt,Judith Masthoff*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出INCLUDE-BENCH，首个大规模评估文本到图像(T2I)模型中残疾相关偏见的基准，包含119K生成图像，评估了17个模型，发现模型普遍存在将残疾固化为轮椅形象、刻板印象突出等系统性偏见。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有T2I模型偏见研究主要关注性别、肤色和文化表征，残疾维度系统性地被忽视，且缺乏基于社会学定义的刻板印象评估框架。

Method: 构建INCLUDE-BENCH基准，包含119K基于多维度提示设计的生成图像，覆盖静态和动态场景；评估15个开源和2个闭源模型；引入刻板印象内容模型(SCM)分数用于量化评估。

Result: (1) 行动障碍和默认残疾提示在所有模型中主要生成轮椅描绘；(2) 残疾条件生成图像多样性显著降低；(3) 刻板印象描绘展示更强的残疾-文本对齐；(4) SCM分数表明T2I模型反映真实世界的刻板印象关联。

Conclusion: T2I模型在残疾表征上存在系统性偏见，需要更严谨的社会学指导的评估框架来识别和缓解向残疾人群体造成的表征性伤害。

Original Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models have been shown to exhibit social biases. Prior work has mainly focused on gender, skin tone, and cultural representation within restricted occupational associations, and emerging benchmarks increasingly incorporate these dimensions. However, disability remains systematically underexplored. Current evaluation practices often fail to align with sociologically grounded definitions of stereotyping, limiting principled assessment of representational harms toward people with disabilities (PWD). To address this, we introduce INCLUDE-BENCH, the first large-scale benchmark for evaluating disability-related bias in T2I models. INCLUDE-BENCH comprises 119K generated images based on prompt design across multiple bias dimensions and both static and dynamic contexts. We evaluate 15 open-source and 2 closed-source models. Our key findings reveal that: (1) mobility-impaired and default disability prompts predominantly yield wheelchair depictions across all models; (2) disability-conditioned generations consistently exhibit less diversity; (3) stereotypical portrayals demonstrate stronger disability-text alignment; and (4) we introduce the Stereotype Content Model (SCM) Score, demonstrating that T2I models reflect real-world stereotypical associations.

中文摘要翻译: 文本到图像（T2I）模型已被证明存在社会偏见。以往研究主要集中在性别、肤色和文化表征在有限职业关联中的问题，新兴基准也越来越多地纳入这些维度。然而，残疾维度系统性地被忽视。当前的评估实践往往未能与社会学定义的刻板印象概念对齐，限制了对残障人士（PWD）表征性伤害的原则性评估。为此，我们引入了INCLUDE-BENCH，这是首个用于评估T2I模型中残疾相关偏见的大规模基准。INCLUDE-BENCH包含基于多维度提示设计的119K张生成图像，覆盖静态和动态场景。我们评估了15个开源模型和2个闭源模型。主要发现表明：(1) 行动障碍和默认残疾提示在所有模型中主要生成轮椅描绘；(2) 残疾条件生成图像的多样性持续降低；(3) 刻板印象描绘表现出更强的残疾-文本对齐；(4) 我们引入了刻板印象内容模型（SCM）分数，证明T2I模型反映了真实世界的刻板印象关联。

</details>


### [55] [Whareformer: Learning to Track What is Where in Long Egocentric Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08537)
*Jacob Chalk,Saptarshi Sinha,Dima Damen,Yannis Kalantidis,Diane Larlus*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Whareformer是首个基于学习的OSNOM任务解决方案，采用Transformer架构，通过可更新的记忆模块和轨迹分配模块，联合推理目标外观和3D位置，仅用56个视频训练便在三个数据集共260个长测试视频上达到SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: OSNOM任务旨在跟踪被摄像者移动的物体，即使它们离开视野或被严重遮挡也要维持实例位置的持续感知。现有方法缺乏学习能力，因此需要提出首个学习型解决方案。

Method: Whareformer是一个基于Transformer的模型，包含两个组件：1）可更新的已建立轨迹记忆模块；2）前馈式的轨迹分配模块，用于将观测与现有轨迹关联。模型联合推理物体外观（what）和更新的3D位置（where），并采用专门的New Track令牌来推理新出现的物体。设计上采用相对距离和演进的轨迹表示。

Result: 仅用56个视频训练，在EPIC-KITCHENS-100（未见过的视频）、IT3DEgo和HD-EPIC三个数据集共260个长测试视频上取得SOTA性能，相比先前工作有显著绝对提升。

Conclusion: Whareformer证明了基于学习的方法在OSNOM任务上的有效性，其设计选择（相对距离、演进轨迹表示、New Track令牌）使得模型能够从小规模训练数据中泛化到大规模测试数据。

Original Abstract: The recently established 'Out of Sight, Not out of Mind' (OSNOM) task for egocentric videos focuses on tracking objects that are moved by the camera wearer, online, maintaining knowledge of instance locations throughout the video even when they leave the field of view or become heavily occluded. In this paper, we propose the first learning-based solution to the OSNOM task: Whareformer, a transformer-based model with two components: an updatable memory of established tracks and a track assignment module that associates observations with existing tracks in a feed-forward manner. Whareformer jointly reasons over evolving object appearance (what) and updated 3D location (where), and employs a dedicated New Track token to reason about novel objects.
  Thanks to its design choices of using relative distances and evolving track representations, Whareformer is trained on a small set of 56 videos but achieves SOTA performance on 260 long test videos from three datasets: EPIC-KITCHENS-100 (unseen videos), IT3DEgo, and HD-EPIC, with significant absolute improvements over prior work.

中文摘要翻译: 最近针对自我中心视频建立的"眼不见，心不念"（OSNOM）任务，旨在在线跟踪被摄像者移动的物体，在视频全程维持实例位置知识，即使物体离开视野或遭受严重遮挡。本文提出了首个基于学习的OSNOM任务解决方案：Whareformer，一个基于Transformer的模型，包含两个组件：一个可更新的已建立轨迹记忆模块，以及一个以前馈方式将观测与现有轨迹关联的轨迹分配模块。Whareformer联合推理演化中的物体外观（what）和更新的3D位置（where），并使用专门的New Track令牌来推理新出现的物体。得益于使用相对距离和演进轨迹表示的设计选择，Whareformer仅用56个视频训练，就在来自三个数据集（EPIC-KITCHENS-100（未见过的视频）、IT3DEgo和HD-EPIC）的260个长测试视频上取得了SOTA性能，相比先前工作有显著的绝对改进。

</details>


### [56] [VocaDet: Sample-Driven Open-Vocabulary Object Detection and Segmentation via Visual Tokenization and Vector Database Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08541)
*ZhiXin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VocaDet提出了一种基于样本驱动的开放词汇目标检测与分割框架，通过将视觉特征转化为离散视觉词汇并存储在可扩展的向量数据库中，无需模型重训练即可从用户提供的正负样本中学习新概念。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放词汇检测方法依赖文本提示、有限视觉样例或昂贵的特征匹配过程，难以扩展到大规模且持续增长的目标库，因此需要一种无需重训练、可灵活扩展的样本驱动方法。

Method: 使用DINOv3作为视觉特征提取器，通过自适应凝聚聚类生成多粒度视觉词汇；结合位置去偏表示和空间拓扑信息存入向量数据库作为可扩展目标记忆；推理时对查询图像进行视觉词汇化并与记忆库高效匹配；引入背景过滤机制减少冗余检索。

Result: 在UA-DETRAC数据集上，VocaDet无需传统检测器训练即实现了有效的开放词汇检测性能，且支持随着正负样本积累而持续扩展的识别能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需重训练、基于检索的开放词汇检测框架，通过视觉词汇化和向量数据库实现可扩展的开放词汇目标识别，为实际场景中的持续学习提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection and segmentation aim to recognize arbitrary objects beyond predefined categories. Although recent vision-language and reference-based approaches have significantly advanced this field, they often rely on text prompts, limited visual examples, or expensive feature matching procedures, making them difficult to scale to large and continuously expanding object repositories. In this work, we propose VocaDet, a sample-driven open-vocabulary object detection and segmentation framework that learns object concepts directly from user-provided positive and negative sample collections without model retraining. The key idea is to transform continuous visual representations into discrete visual vocabularies and perform efficient retrieval-based recognition through a scalable vector database. Specifically, we employ DINOv3 as the visual feature extractor and apply agglomerative clustering with adaptive clustering sensitivity to generate multi-granularity visual tokens. These visual tokens, together with position-debiased representations and spatial topology information, are stored as expandable object memories in a vector database. During inference, query images are converted into visual tokens and efficiently matched against the stored object memories for object localization and segmentation. Furthermore, a background filtering mechanism is introduced to remove frequently occurring background patterns and reduce redundant retrieval operations in practical fixed-camera scenarios. Experiments on the UA-DETRAC dataset demonstrate that VocaDet achieves effective open-vocabulary detection performance without conventional detector training, while supporting continuously expandable recognition capability as additional positive and negative samples are accumulated.

中文摘要翻译: 开放词汇目标检测与分割旨在识别超出预定义类别的任意目标。尽管最近的视觉-语言方法和基于参考的方法显著推动了该领域的发展，但它们往往依赖文本提示、有限的视觉样本或昂贵的特征匹配过程，难以扩展到大规模且持续增长的目标库。在这项工作中，我们提出了VocaDet，一个样本驱动的开放词汇目标检测与分割框架，它直接从用户提供的正负样本集合中学习目标概念，而无需模型重训练。其关键思想是将连续的视觉表示转换为离散的视觉词汇，并通过可扩展的向量数据库进行高效的基于检索的识别。具体而言，我们采用DINOv3作为视觉特征提取器，并应用具有自适应聚类敏感度的凝聚聚类来生成多粒度视觉标记。这些视觉标记连同位置去偏表示和空间拓扑信息，以可扩展的目标记忆形式存储在向量数据库中。在推理过程中，查询图像被转换为视觉标记，并与存储的目标记忆进行高效匹配，以实现目标定位和分割。此外，我们引入了一种背景过滤机制，以去除频繁出现的背景模式，并在实际固定摄像头场景中减少冗余检索操作。在UA-DETRAC数据集上的实验表明，VocaDet无需传统的检测器训练即可实现有效的开放词汇检测性能，同时支持随着正负样本的积累而持续扩展的识别能力。

</details>


### [57] [Switch-Reasoner: Learn When to Think in Multitask Mixtures via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08572)
*Yiyang Fang,Pei Fu,Jinjie Li,Jian Liang,Wenke Huang,Ruijie Luo,Shaojie Zhang,Jian Luan,Yi R. Fung,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Switch-Reasoner框架，让多模态大模型根据输入复杂度自适应选择直接回答或先推理再回答，在11个多模态任务上实现了更好的准确率-效率权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大模型通常采用固定的"先思考再回答"范式，在多任务异构场景下效率低下——简单输入不需要显式推理，而困难输入则能从推理中获益；同时，后训练过程中学习何时推理也不稳定，不平衡的 rollout 会导致模型偏向"始终思考"或"始终直接回答"。

Method: 提出Switch-Reasoner，一种基于GRPO的框架：将思考视为虚拟工具调用，允许模型选择直接回答或先调用推理再回答；引入双层调控机制（dual-level regulation），包括全局平衡思考模式与直接模式的使用比例，以及基于两种选择相对收益的样本级监督。

Result: 在11个多模态任务上的实验表明，Switch-Reasoner在减少不必要推理的同时保持了强性能，实现了更好的准确率-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应推理模式选择方法能有效提升多模态大模型在异构任务场景下的效率，且不牺牲性能。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often follow a fixed Think-then-Answer paradigm, which is inefficient in heterogeneous multitask settings because simple inputs may not require explicit reasoning while difficult ones can benefit substantially from it. Learning when to think is also unstable during post-training, where imbalanced rollouts can drive the model toward always-thinking or always-direct behavior. We propose Switch-Reasoner, a GRPO-based framework that learns to adaptively select reasoning modes for MLLMs. It treats thinking as a virtual tool invocation and allows the model to either answer directly or invoke explicit reasoning before answering. To stabilize this decision, we introduce a dual-level regulation mechanism that balances the overall use of Thinking Mode and Direct Mode while providing sample-level supervision based on the relative benefit of the two choices. Experiments on 11 multimodal tasks show that Switch-Reasoner reduces unnecessary reasoning while maintaining strong performance, achieving a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通常遵循固定的"先思考再回答"范式，这在异构多任务场景中效率低下，因为简单输入可能不需要显式推理，而困难输入则可以从中显著受益。在后训练过程中，学习何时进行推理也不稳定，不平衡的 rollout 会驱使模型走向"始终思考"或"始终直接回答"的行为。我们提出Switch-Reasoner，一种基于GRPO的框架，用于学习自适应地为MLLMs选择推理模式。它将思考视为一种虚拟工具调用，允许模型直接回答或在回答前先调用显式推理。为了稳定这一决策，我们引入了一种双层调控机制，该机制在全局层面平衡思考模式和直接模式的使用，同时基于两种选择的相对收益提供样本级监督。在11个多模态任务上的实验表明，Switch-Reasoner在减少不必要推理的同时保持了强性能，实现了更好的准确率-效率权衡。

</details>


### [58] [When Structured Sparse Autoencoders Learn Consistent Concepts Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08605)
*Weiduo Liao,Yunqiao Yang,Ying Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出结构化稀疏自编码器（S²AE），通过引入基于注意力相似性和空间邻近性的组稀疏正则化，解决视觉-语言模型中稀疏自编码器难以学习模态一致概念的问题，在语义对齐、表示效率和跨模态单义性上取得显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在视觉-语言模型（VLM）中，vanilla SAE难以学习模态一致的概念，视觉模态中的概念往往呈现碎片化覆盖（即不连续的区域），导致跨模态特征表示不连贯。

Method: 提出S²AE：首先基于Transformer注意力相似性和空间邻近性对图像块进行分组，然后在训练vanilla SAE时引入结构化稀疏正则化，包括组间独占稀疏（exclusive sparsity）以实现概念解缠，以及组内组稀疏（group sparsity）以实现概念一致性，驱动SAE的潜在神经元专注于不同且语义可解释的概念。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct模型上，语义对齐（mIoU）平均提升6.06%，表示效率（更低l0范数）提升60.81，重构保真度保持接近完美（解释方差>99%）。跨模态分析显示，语义一致性平均提升3.08%，单义性得分平均提升2.37%。

Conclusion: S²AE通过视觉结构先验有效增强了神经元的单义性，促进了多模态特征更连贯且解缠的表示，为VLM的机械可解释性提供了有效技术手段。

Original Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a promising technique for mechanistic interpretability by learning a set of sparse latent features in large models, each of which encodes a distinct concept. However, in vision-language models (VLMs), vanilla SAEs struggle to learn modality-consistent concepts, with concepts often exhibiting fragmented coverage (i.e., disjoint regions) in the visual modality. To address this challenge, we propose a Structured Sparse AutoEncoder ($S^2AE$) that enforces concept consistency from both semantic and spatial perspectives in the visual modality. Specifically, we group image patches based on Transformer attention similarity and spatial proximity, and introduce a structured sparsity regularization when training the vanilla SAE. The regularization consists of exclusive sparsity for inter-group concept disentanglement and group sparsity for intra-group concept consistency, which drives the latent neurons by SAEs to specialize in distinct, semantically grounded concepts. Evaluated on the \texttt{Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct} model, the method achieves 6.06% average improvement in semantic alignment (mIoU) and 60.81 in representational efficiency (lower l0 norm) while maintaining near-perfect reconstruction fidelity with an Explained Variance above 99%. Cross-modal analysis further demonstrates that $S^2AE$ enhances neuronal monosemanticity by this visual structural prior, achieving a 3.08% average gain in semantic consistency and a 2.37% average gain in monosemanticity scores for both modalities of multimodal features, thereby fostering more coherent and disentangled representations.

中文摘要翻译: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）通过学习大规模模型中的一组稀疏潜在特征（每个特征编码一个独立概念），已成为机械可解释性领域的一种有前景的技术。然而，在视觉-语言模型（VLM）中，vanilla SAE难以学习模态一致的概念，视觉模态中的概念往往呈现碎片化覆盖（即不连续的区域）。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种结构化稀疏自编码器（$S^2AE$），从视觉模态的语义和空间两个角度强制实现概念一致性。具体而言，我们基于Transformer注意力相似性和空间邻近性对图像块进行分组，并在训练vanilla SAE时引入结构化稀疏正则化。该正则化包括用于组间概念解缠的独占稀疏性（exclusive sparsity）和用于组内概念一致性的组稀疏性（group sparsity），从而驱动SAE的潜在神经元专注于不同且语义可解释的概念。在\texttt{Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct}模型上的评估表明，该方法在语义对齐（mIoU）上平均提升6.06%，表示效率（更低的l0范数）提升60.81，同时保持接近完美的重构保真度，解释方差超过99%。跨模态分析进一步表明，$S^2AE$通过这种视觉结构先验增强了神经元的单义性，在多模态特征的两种模态上实现了语义一致性平均提升3.08%和单义性得分平均提升2.37%，从而促进了更连贯且解缠的表示。

</details>


### [59] [Do Transformations Reveal the Truth? Generative Residual Learning for Generalized AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08674)
*Kutub Uddin,Nusrat Tasnim,Awais Khan,Mohammad Umar Farooq,Khalid Malik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GenRes和GenRes++框架，利用神经张量网络和可学习注意力机制，通过生成残差学习来增强AI生成图像检测的泛化能力，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展使得高度逼真的深度伪造媒体可被轻松创建，带来了虚假信息、数字身份盗窃、欺诈和舆论操纵等严重威胁。由于生成方法的多样性和其遗留的细微伪影，AI生成图像检测面临可靠挑战，亟需提升检测模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出GenRes框架，通过神经张量网络对原始样本与变换样本之间的细粒度关系特征进行建模，实现生成残差学习；进一步提出GenRes++，采用可学习注意力机制聚合多个变换样本的关系特征，使模型聚焦于最具信息量的线索。两者均使用PE-Core作为特征提取器，提供泛化且语义丰富的嵌入以提升跨域性能。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的综合实验表明，所提出的GenRes++方法在AI生成图像检测任务上优于现有方法，能够检测由未见生成方法产生的图像。

Conclusion: 本文提出的GenRes和GenRes++框架通过生成残差学习有效提升了AIGI检测的泛化能力，特别是GenRes++借助可学习注意力机制在多个生成变换场景下取得了最优性能，验证了所提方法在跨域和未知生成方法检测方面的有效性。

Original Abstract: The rapid advancement of generative AI has enabled the creation of highly realistic deepfake media, posing significant threats, including misinformation, digital identity theft, fraud, and manipulation of public opinion. AI-generated image (AIGI) detection is reliably challenging due to the diversity of generative methods and the subtle artifacts they leave behind. In this work, we propose GenRes, a novel framework for generative residual learning via a neural tensor network, which models fine-grained relational features between original and transformed samples to enhance generalization. To address scenarios involving multiple generative transformations, we introduce GenRes++, which employs a learnable attention mechanism to aggregate relational features across multiple transformed samples and enables the model to focus on the most informative cues. Both models leverage PE-Core as a feature extractor, providing generalized and semantically rich embeddings that improve cross-domain performance and enable the detection of AIGI generated by unseen methods. Comprehensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed GenRes++ approach outperforms existing methods.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式AI的快速发展使得创建高度逼真的深度伪造媒体成为可能，这对社会构成了重大威胁，包括虚假信息、数字身份盗窃、欺诈以及舆论操纵。由于生成方法的多样性及其遗留的细微伪影，AI生成图像（AIGI）检测面临可靠挑战。在本文中，我们提出GenRes，一种通过神经张量网络进行生成残差学习的新框架，该框架对原始样本与变换样本之间的细粒度关系特征进行建模，从而增强泛化能力。为应对涉及多种生成变换的场景，我们引入GenRes++，它采用可学习注意力机制聚合多个变换样本的关系特征，使模型能够聚焦于最具信息量的线索。两个模型均使用PE-Core作为特征提取器，提供泛化且语义丰富的嵌入，从而提升跨域性能并能够检测由未见生成方法产生的AIGI。在多个基准数据集上的综合实验表明，所提出的GenRes++方法优于现有方法。

</details>


### [60] [Multi-Resolution Feature Stem for Diabetic Retinopathy lesion segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08679)
*Indranil Dutta,Taehee Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了输入分辨率对糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）病灶分割的影响，发现高分辨率虽有利于微动脉瘤检测，却会意外降低大出血病灶的分割性能。为解决这一分辨率权衡问题，提出了一种多分辨率特征茎（Multi-Resolution Feature Stem）架构，将其集成到UNet++骨干网络中，在多个尺度上并行处理图像，实现了细粒度细节与上下文信息的兼顾。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: DR病灶尺度差异巨大（从微小的微动脉瘤到大出血和渗出），高分辨率对微小病灶有利，但可能对大病灶产生负面影响，而现有研究通常假设更高分辨率总是有益。本文旨在系统探究输入分辨率对不同类型病灶的影响，并解决这一矛盾需求。

Method: 提出了一种多分辨率特征茎（Multi-Resolution Feature Stem），即输入级图像金字塔，将其集成到UNet++骨干网络中。该架构在多个尺度上并行处理输入图像，同时捕获细粒度细节和全局上下文信息，参数效率高。

Result: 通过U-Net、UNet++、Vision Transformers、DeepLabV3+等多种架构在512×512和1024×1024分辨率下的系统实验，发现了一个关键的反直觉现象：提高输入分辨率对不同类型的病灶具有相反的效果——更高分辨率对解决细微的微动脉瘤至关重要，但会意外降低大出血病灶的分割性能。

Conclusion: 本文提供了关于分辨率依赖性行为的关键实证证据，并提出了一种实用且参数高效的架构，成功解决了分辨率权衡问题。

Original Abstract: Diabetic Retinopathy (DR) is a leading cause of preventable blindness worldwide, requiring automated lesion segmentation using deep learning models for early detection and monitoring. However, DR lesions vary dramatically in size from tiny microaneurysms to large hemorrhages and exudates. This variability creates conflicting demands on the model architecture and input resolution, posing a challenge for effective design. This work investigates the impact of input resolution on different lesion types. Through systematic experimentation with multiple architectures (U-Net, UNet++, Vision Transformers, DeepLabV3+) at $512 \times 512$ and $1024 \times 1024$ resolutions, we identify a critical, counter-intuitive phenomenon where increasing input resolution has opposing effects on different lesion types. We demonstrate that while higher resolution is essential for resolving fine-grained microaneurysms, it can unexpectedly degrade performance on larger hemorrhages. This finding challenges the common assumption that higher resolution is uniformly beneficial. To address this, we propose a novel Multi-Resolution Feature Stem, an input-level pyramid integrated with a UNet++ backbone. This architecture processes multiple scales in parallel, capturing fine-grained details without sacrificing contextual information. This work contributes crucial empirical evidence of this complex, resolution-dependent behavior and a practical, parameter-efficient architecture that successfully resolves this trade-off.
