Original Abstract: Male infertility is a significant yet often underdiagnosed aspect of reproductive health, with semen analysis serving as the cornerstone of clinical evaluation. To address this problem, this study investigates the use of machine learning algorithms to classify male fertility status based on key semen parameters, i.e., sperm concentration, motility, and morphology, using the VISEM dataset. This dataset includes semen samples from 85 participants, classified into three categories, i.e., Fertile, Sub-Fertile, and Infertile, according to the World Health Organization's criteria. After pre-processing and feature engineering, the dataset was used to train and assess multiple classification models using the LazyPredict framework. Among the more than 40 algorithms tested, the Nearest Centroid classifier achieved an accuracy of 94.2%, outperforming other models such as Support Vector Machines and Quadratic Discriminant Analysis. The model's robustness was validated using 5-fold cross-validation and multiclass ROC-AUC analysis. This study illustrates that machine learning models can provide fast, accurate, and objective assessments of semen quality, potentially supporting clinical decision-making in andrology and assisted reproductive technologies. These findings emphasize the growing potential of machine learning to enhance fertility diagnostics and inform patient-specific treatment strategies.

中文摘要翻译: 男性不育是生殖健康中一个重要但常被低估的方面，精液分析是临床评估的基石。针对这一问题，本研究探讨了利用机器学习算法基于关键精液参数（即精子浓度、活力和形态）对男性生育状态进行分类的方法，使用了VISEM数据集。该数据集包含85名参与者的精液样本，根据世界卫生组织标准分为三类：可育、次育和不育。经过预处理和特征工程后，利用LazyPredict框架对多种分类模型进行训练和评估。在测试的40多种算法中，最近质心分类器取得了94.2%的准确率，优于支持向量机和二次判别分析等其他模型。通过5折交叉验证和多分类ROC-AUC分析验证了模型的鲁棒性。本研究表明，机器学习模型能够提供快速、准确、客观的精液质量评估，有望为男科学和辅助生殖技术中的临床决策提供支持。这些发现强调了机器学习在增强生育力诊断和为患者个体化治疗策略提供信息方面的日益增长的潜力。

</details>


### [151] [MatBind: A Shared Embedding Space for Multimodal Materials Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08470)
*Le Yang,Anoop K. Chandran,Jona Östreicher,Evgenii Sovetkin,Adrian Mirza,Sebastien Bompas,Bashir Kazimi,Pascal Friederich,Stefan Kesselheim,Kevin Maik Jablonka,Stefan Sandfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MatBind 是一个对比学习框架，将晶体结构、X射线衍射、电子态密度和文本四种材料模态对齐到统一嵌入空间，以晶体结构为中心锚点，实现零样本跨模态检索。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 材料科学中，不同模态的数据（原子结构、衍射图谱、态密度、自然语言）各自描述同一物理对象的不同侧面，但现有方法将这些模态孤立存储和分析，难以跨表征边界进行关联或查询。

Method: 提出MatBind对比学习框架，以晶体结构为物理中心锚点，将四种材料模态（晶体结构、模拟粉末X射线衍射、态密度、文本）对齐到统一嵌入空间。

Result: 嵌入空间能够根据物理意义组织材料而无需显式监督；在查询时组合多种模态可系统性地提升检索性能；实现了训练中从未显式配对的模态之间的零样本跨模态检索。

Conclusion: 将异质材料数据视为同一物理实在的互补投影（而非孤立数据源）不仅是一种实践选择，更与底层物理规律一致。

Original Abstract: Fully characterizing a crystalline material requires integrating heterogeneous data sources -- atomic structures, diffraction patterns, electronic density of states, and natural language -- each of which captures a different facet of the same physical object. In practice, however, these modalities are stored and analyzed in isolation, making it difficult to relate or query materials across representational boundaries. We present MatBind, a contrastive learning framework that aligns four materials modalities -- crystal structure, powder X-ray diffraction (pXRD) simulated from structures, density of states (DOS), and text -- into a unified embedding space using crystal structure as the central physical anchor. The framework induces alignment between modalities never explicitly paired during training, enabling emergent zero-shot cross-modal retrieval as a direct consequence of the shared representation. The learned embedding space organizes materials according to physically meaningful properties without explicit supervision, and retrieval performance improves systematically when modalities are combined at query time. These results demonstrate that treating heterogeneous materials data as complementary projections of a single physical reality, rather than as isolated data sources, is not a practical choice but is consistent with the underlying physics.

中文摘要翻译: 完整表征一种晶体材料需要整合异构数据源——原子结构、衍射图谱、电子态密度以及自然语言文本——每种模态都捕捉同一物理对象的不同侧面。然而在实践中，这些模态被孤立地存储和分析，导致难以跨表征边界关联或查询材料。我们提出了MatBind，一个对比学习框架，它将四种材料模态——晶体结构、基于结构模拟的粉末X射线衍射（pXRD）、态密度（DOS）和文本——以晶体结构作为物理中心锚点，对齐到统一的嵌入空间中。该框架促使训练中从未显式配对的模态之间产生对齐，从而实现了零样本跨模态检索，这是共享表征的直接结果。学习到的嵌入空间无需显式监督即可根据物理有意义的属性组织材料，并且在查询时组合多种模态可系统性地提升检索性能。这些结果表明，将异质材料数据视为同一物理实在的互补投影（而非孤立的数据源），不仅是一种实践选择，更与底层物理规律相一致。

</details>


### [152] [Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality Transportation Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08475)
*Jiewen Deng,Hangchen Liu,Junchen Li,Boyuan Zhang,Renhe Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FreMo，一种轻量级的频域多模态交通预测模型，通过显式利用频域信息实现自适应、选择性的跨模态协同，在多个真实数据集上超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法主要在时域操作或依赖粗粒度的特征融合，无法有效处理不同交通模态在频谱特性上的差异以及它们在不同频率上的不均匀交互，导致多模态交通预测精度受限。

Method: FreMo包含两个核心模块：(1) 模态级频率滤波器（MFF），自适应精炼各模态内的频谱成分，增强信息频率并抑制噪声；(2) 频率引导的协同集成器（FSI），基于各频率上不同模态的相对贡献选择性聚合信息，实现有效的跨模态知识共享并减少负迁移。模型支持即插即用，可集成到通用时间序列骨干网络中。

Result: 在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，FreMo在多种预测场景下一致性地优于最先进的基线方法，展现出优越的性能和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式利用频域信息进行模态级频谱精炼与跨模态选择性协同，是一种轻量且有效的多模态交通预测范式。

Original Abstract: Multi-modality transportation refers to urban systems composed of multiple transportation modes, such as traffic flow and public transit, whose dynamics are coupled by shared temporal patterns. Accurate multi-modality transportation forecasting remains challenging because (1) different modalities exhibit distinct spectral characteristics and (2) interact unevenly across frequencies, whereas most existing methods operate primarily in the time domain or rely on coarse feature fusion. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight yet effective Frequency-Domain Multi-Modality modeling (FreMo) that explicitly exploits the frequency domain to enable adaptive and selective cross-modality synergy. FreMo disentangles modality-wise spectral refinement from cross-modality synergy and supports plug-and-play integration with general time series backbones. Specifically, FreMo introduces a Modality-Wise Frequency Filter (MFF) to adaptively refine spectral components within each modality, emphasizing informative frequencies while suppressing noise. FreMo further incorporates a Frequency-Guided Synergy Integrator (FSI) that selectively aggregates information across modalities based on their relative contribution at each frequency, facilitating effective cross-modality knowledge sharing while mitigating negative transfer. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets show that FreMo consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with superior performance and generalization across diverse forecasting scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/beginner-sketch/FreMo.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态交通是指由多种交通方式（如交通流和公共交通）组成的城市系统，其动态通过共享的时间模式相互耦合。准确的多模态交通预测仍然具有挑战性，因为（1）不同模态表现出不同的频谱特性，（2）在不同频率上存在不均匀的交互，而现有方法主要在时域操作或依赖粗粒度的特征融合。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了一种轻量级且有效的频域多模态建模方法（FreMo），该方法显式利用频域来实现自适应和选择性的跨模态协同。FreMo将模态级频谱精炼与跨模态协同解耦，并支持与通用时间序列骨干网络的即插即用集成。具体来说，FreMo引入了一个模态级频率滤波器（MFF），自适应地精炼每个模态内的频谱成分，增强信息频率同时抑制噪声。FreMo还引入了一个频率引导的协同集成器（FSI），基于各频率上不同模态的相对贡献选择性聚合信息，促进有效的跨模态知识共享并减轻负迁移。在真实数据集上的大量实验表明，FreMo在多种预测场景下一致性地优于最先进的基线方法，具有优越的性能和泛化能力。代码已在 https://github.com/beginner-sketch/FreMo 上公开。

</details>


### [153] [Ensemble Diversity Optimization for Subjective Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08493)
*Xia Cui,Ziyi Huang,N. R. Abeynayake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出集成多样性优化（EDO）框架，通过联合优化集成权重、有效基数和校准，在有主观性的NLP分类任务中建模标注者分歧，提升概率校准性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 主观性NLP任务存在系统性标注者分歧，现有模型往往忽视或压制这种不确定性，需要能够保留而非消除不确定性的建模方法。

Method: EDO是一个预测空间框架，通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛实现端到端的集成组成和大小学习，引入符号多样性正则化器（在验证集上调优）以控制保留或抑制分歧，包含软F1代理损失、类别加权交叉熵和可靠性加权多样性，通过统一可微目标联合优化集成权重、有效基数和校准。

Result: 在四个主观文本分类基准（ArMIS、ConvAbuse、HS-Brexit、MD-Agreement）上，EDO相比Soft-CE、Soft-MD、Top-5 Voting和WEL基线，交叉熵降低40-78%，Brier分数降低，同时保持有竞争力的F1分数，并与标注者分布更好对齐。

Conclusion: 联合优化集成结构与符号多样性正则化器是一种高效、模型无关的方法，能够在监督学习中有效建模人类主观性，在保持分类性能的同时显著提升概率校准质量。

Original Abstract: Subjective NLP tasks often exhibit systematic annotator disagreement, requiring models that represent uncertainty rather than collapse it. We introduce Ensemble Diversity Optimization (EDO), a prediction-space framework that jointly optimizes ensemble weights, effective cardinality, and calibration through a unified differentiable objective. EDO learns ensemble composition and size end-to-end via Gumbel-Softmax relaxation and incorporates a signed diversity regularizer, tuned on validation data, to steer optimization toward either preserving or suppressing disagreement. This regularization prevents ensemble collapse and enables controlled navigation of the utility-calibration trade-off. The framework integrates a soft F1 surrogate, class-weighted cross-entropy to address imbalance, and reliability-weighted diversity to regulate intra-ensemble variability. Experiments on four subjective text-classification benchmarks (ArMIS, ConvAbuse, HS-Brexit, MD-Agreement) show that EDO substantially improves probabilistic calibration, reducing cross-entropy (40-78% depending on baseline) and lowering Brier scores relative to Soft-CE, Soft-MD, Top-5 Voting, and WEL, while maintaining competitive F1 and better alignment with annotator distributions. These results demonstrate that jointly optimizing ensemble structure with a signed diversity regularizer provides an efficient, model-agnostic approach for modeling human subjectivity in supervised learning.

中文摘要翻译: 主观性自然语言处理任务通常表现出系统性的标注者分歧，这要求模型能够表示不确定性而非将其消除。我们提出集成多样性优化（EDO），这是一个预测空间框架，通过统一可微目标联合优化集成权重、有效基数和校准。EDO通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛以端到端方式学习集成组成和大小，并引入一个在验证集上调优的符号多样性正则化器，以引导优化朝向保留或抑制分歧。该正则化防止集成坍塌，并实现对效用-校准权衡的可控导航。该框架集成了软F1代理损失、用于处理类别不平衡的类别加权交叉熵，以及用于调节集成内部变异性的可靠性加权多样性。在四个主观文本分类基准（ArMIS、ConvAbuse、HS-Brexit、MD-Agreement）上的实验表明，EDO显著改善了概率校准，相对于Soft-CE、Soft-MD、Top-5 Voting和WEL基线，交叉熵降低了40%-78%，Brier分数也得以降低，同时保持了有竞争力的F1分数并与标注者分布更好地对齐。这些结果表明，联合优化集成结构与符号多样性正则化器为在监督学习中建模人类主观性提供了一种高效、模型无关的方法。

</details>


### [154] [Systematic Evaluation of Learning Rate Scheduling Strategies Across Heterogeneous Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08511)
*Hafsa Mateen,Radu Timofte,Dmitry Ignatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了学习率调度策略对神经网络分类准确率的影响，通过在LEMUR数据集上对30种架构、25种调度配置进行大规模实验（共3,938个模型变体），发现调度器的选择高度依赖于架构，其中CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR持续优于基础衰减策略，最佳配置在CIFAR-10上达到86.45%的top-1准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 学习率调度策略对神经网络训练至关重要，但手动选择成本高昂且难以穷举，而传统AutoML方法通常将调度器视为次要超参数，缺乏对其影响的系统性研究。

Method: 从LEMUR神经网络数据集中选取30种代表性架构（来自卷积和Transformer系列），通过自动化源代码注入方式，应用来自9个PyTorch调度器家族的25种配置，在CIFAR-10上评估了总共3,938个模型变体。

Result: 最佳配置在CIFAR-10上达到86.45%的top-1准确率，其中237个变体超过80%准确率。结果表明调度器的选择高度依赖于架构，CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR持续优于基础衰减策略。

Conclusion: 学习率调度器的选择对分类准确率有显著影响，且该影响与架构高度相关；研究结果形成的准确率景观可作为调度器选择的原则性参考。

Original Abstract: Choosing a learning rate scheduling strategy is critical to neural network training, but manual selection is costly and rarely exhaustive. While classical AutoML approaches often treat the scheduler as a secondary hyperparameter, we systematically investigate its impact on classification accuracy across a diverse pool of architectures. We evaluated 30 representative architectures from convolutional and transformer families within the LEMUR neural network dataset. Through automated source-code injection, we applied 25 scheduler configurations across nine PyTorch families, evaluating a total of 3,938 model variants on CIFAR-10. Our best configuration achieved a top-1 accuracy of 86.45%, with 237 variants exceeding 80%. The results show that the choice of scheduler depends heavily on the architecture: CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts and CyclicLR consistently outperform basic decay strategies. The resulting accuracy landscape, contributed to the LEMUR nn-dataset, provides a practical reference for principled scheduler selection.

中文摘要翻译: 选择学习率调度策略对神经网络训练至关重要，但手动选择成本高昂且难以做到穷举。虽然经典的AutoML方法通常将调度器视为次要超参数，但我们系统性地研究了调度器对不同架构池中分类准确率的影响。我们从LEMUR神经网络数据集中选取了30种来自卷积和Transformer系列的代表性架构进行评估。通过自动化源代码注入，我们应用了来自9个PyTorch调度器家族的25种配置，在CIFAR-10上共评估了3,938个模型变体。我们的最佳配置达到了86.45%的top-1准确率，其中有237个变体准确率超过80%。结果表明，调度器的选择在很大程度上依赖于架构：CosineAnnealingWarmRestarts和CyclicLR持续优于基础衰减策略。由此产生的准确率景观已贡献到LEMUR nn-dataset中，为原则性的调度器选择提供了实用参考。

</details>


### [155] [Stop Guessing When to Stop Testing: Efficient Model Evaluation with Just Enough Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08522)
*Ofir Arviv,Kristjan Greenewald,Yotam Perlitz,Hadar Mulian,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出采用序贯测试替代固定样本量评估，构建自适应评估框架以平衡模型评估中的效率与可靠性，在Open VLM排行榜上实现了80%的计算成本降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 固定大小基准的刚性导致其无法高效适配模型评估中多样化的统计效力需求（如模型排名、选择、开发测试等），造成计算成本过高或可靠性不足的问题。

Method: 将序贯测试这一经典统计范式与针对常见评估需求定制的停止准则（如收益递减检测、最小可检测效应量）相结合，构建自适应评估框架。

Result: 在Open VLM排行榜上，与固定大小评估相比，在保持统计显著性的前提下，实现了高达80%的计算成本削减（允许2.5分置信区间宽度）。

Conclusion: 序贯测试能够有效解决固定样本量评估的刚性问题，为模型评估提供一种原则性的自适应方法，在效率与可靠性之间实现动态平衡。

Original Abstract: The inherent rigidity of fixed-size benchmarks makes them an inefficient tool for model evaluation. Diverse evaluation objectives, including model ranking, model selection and testing throughout development, demand varying levels of statistical power. The mismatch between fixed sample sizes and these diverse needs results in either excessive computational cost or compromised reliability - a critical concern for model evaluation. To overcome these limitations, we call for adoption of sequential testing in our field. We provide an adaptive evaluation framework, that provides a principled way to navigate the trade-off between efficiency and reliability in model evaluation. Our framework combines the established statistical paradigm of sequential testing with stopping criteria tailored to common evaluation needs such as diminishing returns detection, and minimum detectable effect size. We demonstrate its ability to adaptively manage the efficiency-reliability trade-off on the Open VLM Leaderboard, including, for example, a 80% reduction in computational cost compared to fixed-size evaluation (with a 2.5-point CI width allowance) while maintaining statistical significance.

中文摘要翻译: 固定大小基准的固有刚性使其成为低效的模型评估工具。多样化的评估目标——包括模型排名、模型选择以及开发过程中的测试——对统计效力提出了不同层次的需求。固定样本量与这些多样化需求之间的不匹配，要么导致过高的计算成本，要么损害可靠性——这是模型评估中的一个关键问题。为克服这些局限性，我们呼吁在该领域采用序贯测试。我们提供了一个自适应评估框架，该框架提供了一种原则性的方法来导航模型评估中效率与可靠性之间的权衡。我们的框架将经典的序贯测试统计范式与针对常见评估需求定制的停止准则相结合，例如收益递减检测和最小可检测效应量。我们在Open VLM排行榜上展示了该框架自适应管理效率-可靠性权衡的能力，例如，与固定大小评估相比，在保持统计显著性的同时减少了80%的计算成本（允许2.5分的置信区间宽度）。

</details>


### [156] [CAAD: Causality-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Alignment and Structural Causal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08555)
*Xin Wang,Yunshi Wen,Yanan He,Haotian Xu,Youlan Zhao,Michel Ferreira Cardia Haddad,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CAAD框架，通过验证格兰杰因果一致性进行异常检测，利用外生变量残差建模和梯度矩阵监控因果关系统计，在工业数据集上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法仅关注表示的时间相似性，忽略了系统故障和潜在异常表征中的内部因果关系破坏。

Method: CAAD框架将外生时间序列变量建模为残差，通过多尺度对齐内化系统动态，利用梯度基矩阵监控内部因果关系统计，量化动态演化和关系拓扑的因果偏差。

Result: 在真实世界工业数据集上的大量实验表明，CAAD实现了高精度异常检测，优于大多数最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: CAAD通过因果一致性验证有效捕捉细微因果偏移，实现精确异常检测。

Original Abstract: The operational integrity of complex industrial systems relies on precise anomaly detection and diagnosis. The vast majority of existing methods narrowly focus on capturing temporal similarities of representations, often overlooking the disruption of internal causal relationships, which characterizes system failures and latent anomalies. In this paper, we propose a novel framework (CAAD) that reframes anomaly detection as the continuous verification of Granger causality consistency through exogenous variables.
  Specifically, the CAAD framework models exogenous time-series variables as residuals, identifying anomalies as significant deviations caused by external interventions. The proposed framework leverages multi-scale alignment to internalize system dynamics and utilizes a gradient-based matrix to monitor internal causal relationship breakdowns. By quantifying causal deviations of both dynamic evolution and relational topology, the CAAD is able to capture subtle causal shifts to achieve precise anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on real-world industrial datasets demonstrate that the CAAD achieves high-precision anomaly detection, outperforming most state-of-the-art baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 复杂工业系统的运行完整性依赖于精确的异常检测与诊断。现有绝大多数方法狭隘地专注于捕捉表示的时间相似性，往往忽略了内部因果关系的破坏——而这正是系统故障与潜在异常的表征特征。本文提出一个新颖的CAAD框架，将异常检测重新定义为通过外生变量对格兰杰因果一致性进行持续验证。具体而言，CAAD框架将外生时间序列变量建模为残差，将异常识别为外部干预导致的显著偏离。该框架利用多尺度对齐来内化系统动态，并运用基于梯度的矩阵监控内部因果关系的解体。通过量化动态演化与关系拓扑的因果偏差，CAAD能够捕捉细微的因果偏移，实现精确的异常检测。在真实工业数据集上的大量实验表明，CAAD实现了高精度异常检测，优于大多数最先进的基线方法。

</details>


### [157] [Spectral Stability of Pseudoinverse-Based Extreme Learning Machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08581)
*Bich Van Nguyen,Ngoc Anh Khong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从谱分析角度研究基于伪逆的极限学习机（ELM），揭示隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构（特别是最小奇异值和条件数）是影响ELM数值稳定性的根本因素，并实验证明SVD方法在病态条件下比迭代方法更可靠。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的ELM虽训练速度快，但其基于Moore-Penrose伪逆的求解方式受隐藏层矩阵条件数影响显著，数值稳定性缺乏系统性的谱分析理解。

Method: 从谱分析视角研究伪逆ELM，分析最小奇异值对输出权重扰动放大的影响，比较基于SVD的伪逆计算与迭代超幂方法，并通过随机特征解释讨论宽度相关的条件数问题。

Result: SVD方法在病态条件下仍然最可靠，迭代方法对谱特性更敏感；ELM的稳定性根本上由隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构决定。

Conclusion: ELM的数值稳定性由其隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构根本性决定，SVD方法是处理病态条件时最可靠的选择。

Original Abstract: Extreme Learning Machine (ELM) computes output weights analytically using the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse. Although this leads to fast training, its numerical stability depends strongly on the conditioning of the hidden layer matrix. This paper studies pseudoinverse-based ELM from a spectral perspective. We show that the smallest singular value governs perturbation amplification in the output weights, while the condition number provides a quantitative measure of hidden-layer instability. We compare SVD-based pseudoinverse computation with iterative hyperpower methods and discuss width-dependent conditioning through a random feature interpretation. Experiments on synthetic matrices and ELM benchmarks show that SVD-based methods remain the most reliable under ill conditioning, while iterative methods are more sensitive to spectral properties. The results suggest that ELM stability is fundamentally governed by the singular value structure of the hidden layer matrix.

中文摘要翻译: 极限学习机（ELM）通过Moore-Penrose伪逆解析计算输出权重。尽管这带来了快速的训练速度，但其数值稳定性强烈依赖于隐藏层矩阵的条件数。本文从谱分析角度研究基于伪逆的ELM。我们表明，最小奇异值决定了输出权重的扰动放大程度，而条件数则提供了隐藏层不稳定性的定量度量。我们比较了基于SVD的伪逆计算与迭代超幂方法，并通过随机特征解释讨论了宽度相关的条件数问题。在合成矩阵和ELM基准上的实验表明，SVD方法在病态条件下仍然最可靠，而迭代方法对谱特性更敏感。结果表明，ELM的稳定性根本上由隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构决定。

</details>


### [158] [Robust Bayesian Decision Making under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08590)
*Haripriya Harikumar,Sammie Katt,Yasir Zubayr Barlas,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种对抗鲁棒的决策感知实验设计方法，在数据采集时考虑对抗性变量的最坏情况影响，以提升下游决策的稳定性，而非仅仅追求名义最优性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有决策感知实验设计方法通常假设模型设定正确，并依赖最优决策在现实扰动下的稳定性。然而实践中，实验结果常受隐藏或弱建模效应影响，可能显著改变决策最优性，导致误导性结论。

Method: 基于贝叶斯决策理论，形式化了对抗鲁棒的最优决策，并推导出 principled 的贝叶斯实验设计准则，该准则明确以决策稳定性而非名义最优性为目标。

Result: 在合成和真实科学数据集上的实验表明，传统决策感知设计可能快速收敛到高置信度但脆弱的决策，而所提鲁棒感知方法在对抗性变化下能产生显著更稳定可靠的决策。

Conclusion: 对抗鲁棒的实验设计能够有效提升决策在未建模或隐藏效应下的稳定性，是一种更可靠的下游决策支持方法。

Original Abstract: Scientific experiments are often designed to maximize information gain, yet in many applications the primary objective is to support reliable downstream decision-making. Existing decision-aware experimental design and active learning methods typically assume well-specified outcome models and implicitly rely on the stability of the optimal decision under real-world perturbations. In practice, however, experimental outcomes are frequently influenced by hidden or weakly modeled effects, which can substantially alter decision optimality and lead to misleading conclusions. We study sequential adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design, where data acquisition has to take into account information gain against plausible worst-case unexpected effects, modeled here as variation in adversarial variables. Building on Bayesian decision theory, we formalize an adversarially robust optimal decision under this setting and derive a principled Bayesian experimental design criterion. The criterion explicitly targets decision stability rather than nominal optimality. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific datasets show that conventional decision-aware design can converge rapidly to high confidence yet fragile decisions, while our robustness-aware approach yields decisions that are significantly more stable and reliable under adversarial variation.

中文摘要翻译: 科学实验通常旨在最大化信息增益，然而在许多应用中，主要目标是支持可靠的下游决策。现有的决策感知实验设计和主动学习方法通常假设结果模型设定正确，并隐含依赖最优决策在现实扰动下的稳定性。然而在实践中，实验结果经常受到隐藏或弱建模效应的影响，这些效应可能显著改变决策的最优性并导致误导性结论。本文研究了序贯对抗鲁棒决策感知实验设计问题，其中数据获取需考虑对抗最坏情况意外效应（本文建模为对抗性变量的变化）下的信息增益。基于贝叶斯决策理论，我们形式化了该设定下的对抗鲁棒最优决策，并推导出一个原则性的贝叶斯实验设计准则。该准则明确以决策稳定性而非名义最优性为目标。在合成和真实科学数据集上的实验表明，传统的决策感知设计可能快速收敛到高置信度但脆弱的决策，而我们的鲁棒感知方法在对抗性变化下能产生显著更稳定可靠的决策。

</details>


### [159] [Federated Deep Learning for Privacy-Preserving Cardiovascular Disease Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08595)
*Hyunho Mo,Djura Smits,Mahlet A. Birhanu,Maarten J. G. Leening,Daniel Bos,Pim van der Harst,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种联邦深度学习方法，用于在保护隐私的前提下整合两个异质性人群队列（Lifelines和Rotterdam Study）进行心血管疾病风险预测。实验表明，联邦学习训练的深度生存模型在C-statistic上优于本地训练模型，证明了跨异质队列的联邦学习可在保护患者数据隐私的同时提升预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 心血管疾病风险预测模型通常依赖单机构或集中式数据集，但由于隐私法规和数据共享限制，跨机构扩展存在困难。联邦学习虽能解决数据隐私问题，但在医疗场景中因数据集在规模、人群特征和结果定义上的异质性而面临挑战。本文旨在探索联邦深度学习是否能在异质人群队列中实现隐私保护下的心血管疾病风险预测改进。

Method: 采用联邦深度学习方法，整合两个异质性人群队列：Lifelines（148,230名参与者，自报告结局）和Rotterdam Study（10,155名参与者，临床数字化链接结局）。使用深度生存模型在联邦学习框架下进行训练，在保护各机构数据不泄露的前提下协同优化模型参数。

Result: 在Rotterdam Study上，联邦学习训练的C-statistic从0.728（95% CI: 0.717-0.739）提升至0.739（95% CI: 0.728-0.749）；在Lifelines上，C-statistic从0.783（95% CI: 0.775-0.791）提升至0.787（95% CI: 0.780-0.792）。模型性能主要在Rotterdam Study上评估，因其具有完整的随访数据。

Conclusion: 跨异质性队列的联邦深度学习能够改善心血管疾病风险预测，同时保护个体水平患者数据的隐私。

Original Abstract: Cardiovascular disease risk prediction models often rely on data from a single institution or centrally pooled datasets. Extending these models across institutions could be limited by privacy regulations and constraints on sharing patient-level data. Federated learning enables collaborative model development without transferring sensitive patient data, but its application in healthcare remains challenging because datasets often differ in size, population characteristics, and outcome definitions. In this study, we present a federated deep learning approach for privacy-preserving cardiovascular disease risk prediction that integrates two population-based cohorts with different characteristics: Lifelines, including 148,230 participants meeting the study inclusion criteria with self-reported outcomes, and the Rotterdam Study, including a smaller cohort of 10,155 participants with digitally linked clinical outcomes. Model performance was primarily evaluated on the Rotterdam Study because of its complete follow-up. Deep survival models trained using federated learning achieved higher predictive performance than models trained locally without federation. For the Rotterdam Study, the C-statistic increased from 0.728 (95% CI: 0.717-0.739) to 0.739 (95% CI: 0.728-0.749). For Lifelines, the C-statistic increased from 0.783 (95% CI: 0.775-0.791) to 0.787 (95% CI: 0.780-0.792). These findings suggest that federated deep learning across heterogeneous cohorts can improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while preserving the privacy of individual-level patient data.

中文摘要翻译: 心血管疾病风险预测模型通常依赖于来自单一机构或集中汇集的数据库。将这些模型扩展到跨机构应用可能受到隐私法规和患者级数据共享限制的制约。联邦学习能够在无需传输敏感患者数据的情况下实现协作式模型开发，但其在医疗领域的应用仍具挑战性，因为数据集在规模、人群特征和结局定义上往往存在差异。在本研究中，我们提出了一种用于隐私保护性心血管疾病风险预测的联邦深度学习方法，该方法整合了两个具有不同特征的人群队列：Lifelines（包含148,230名符合研究纳入标准并提供自报告结局的参与者）和Rotterdam Study（包含一个较小的队列，共10,155名具有数字化链接临床结局的参与者）。由于Rotterdam Study具有完整的随访数据，模型性能主要在该队列上进行评估。使用联邦学习训练的深度生存模型比未使用联邦学习而仅在本地训练的模型获得了更高的预测性能。对于Rotterdam Study，C-statistic从0.728（95% CI: 0.717-0.739）提升至0.739（95% CI: 0.728-0.749）。对于Lifelines，C-statistic从0.783（95% CI: 0.775-0.791）提升至0.787（95% CI: 0.780-0.792）。这些发现表明，跨异质性队列的联邦深度学习可以在保护个体水平患者数据隐私的同时，改善心血管疾病风险预测。

</details>


### [160] [Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08641)
*Yann Claes,Pierre Geurts,Vân Anh Huynh-Thu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于偏依赖的神经网络引导方法，使模型对特定特征的平均响应符合领域知识，在回归问题（尤其是动力系统预测）上比无约束模型更优且更数据高效。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有解释引导学习多聚焦分类问题，假设已知重要输入特征/区域，且缺乏在回归问题中利用偏依赖知识引导模型训练的方法。

Method: 基于偏依赖（partial dependence）引导神经网络训练，使模型对特定特征的平均响应与用户提供的函数领域知识一致。

Result: 在多个回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上，经该方法控制的模型性能优于无约束模型，且数据效率更高；其解释与用户知识一致，而未经控制的模型则不然。

Conclusion: 基于偏依赖的引导学习方法能有效将领域知识融入神经网络训练，提升回归任务的性能与可解释性。

Original Abstract: Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.

中文摘要翻译: 过去几年，人们对提高机器学习模型的可解释性越来越感兴趣。尽管大量工作致力于开发解释模型所学交互关系的技术，但较少研究关注评估这些解释的质量。更少研究关注如何调整模型以产生忠实于先验知识的解释，这一过程被称为解释引导学习。此外，该领域的大多数方法聚焦于分类问题，并通常假设已知哪些输入特征或区域最为重要。在这项工作中，我们引入了一种基于偏依赖的神经网络引导新方法，使得模型对某些特征的平均响应与关于该问题的特定函数领域知识相一致。我们在包括动力系统预测在内的一系列回归问题上进行了实证验证，结果表明，使用我们的方法控制训练过程的模型性能优于无约束模型，且数据效率更高。此外，我们强调，前者获得的解释实际上与用户提供的知识一致，而后者获得的解释则不然。

</details>


### [161] [BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08643)
*Yuantian Shao,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Chuangyi Li,Yuanteng Chen,Pengcheng Xie,Yiwu Yao,Zhihui Wei,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiSCo-LLM提出了一种无码本的二进制球面编码框架，用于极端低位LLM权重压缩，通过球面编码、残差BSQ和类别级恢复蒸馏实现高效压缩，无需显式存储码本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有低位压缩方法中，标量/分组量化在2比特权重预算下表示能力有限，而向量量化虽表示能力强但需要显式码本、索引查找和额外存储开销。因此需要一种既具有丰富表示能力又无需码本存储的极端低位压缩方法。

Method: 提出BiSCo-LLM框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 将局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为紧致球面编码，有效载荷为比特打包的符号流而非显式VQ质心；(2) 残差BSQ阶段编码基球面编解码器遗留的重建误差，提供显式率失真路径且无需存储码本；(3) 每个Transformer模块类别替换后进行类别级恢复蒸馏，减少局部权重重建与组装模型行为之间的不匹配。此外使用8比特保护通道路径作为敏感通道的辅助稳定机制。

Result: 报告的存储预算包括二进制编码、神经解码器、保护通道有效载荷、LoRA适配器和元数据，实现了极端低位压缩下的高效模型部署。

Conclusion: BiSCo-LLM通过无码本的二进制球面编码框架，在极端低位权重压缩场景下兼顾了表示能力与存储效率，为LLM部署中的内存和带宽瓶颈提供了有效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）在部署过程中日益受到内存容量、权重带宽和检查点存储的限制。现有的低位压缩方法主要遵循两个方向。标量或分组量化简单且兼容高效的低精度内核，但当目标预算接近每权重2比特时，其表示能力变得有限。向量量化的权重压缩提供了更丰富的块级表示，但通常引入显式码本、索引查找和额外的存储开销。本文提出BiSCo-LLM，一种无码本的二进制球面编码框架，用于极端低位LLM权重压缩。核心流程建立在三个组件之上。首先，将局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为紧致球面编码，使得主要有效载荷是比特打包的符号流而非显式VQ质心。其次，残差BSQ阶段编码基球面编解码器留下的重建误差，提供无需存储码本的显式率失真路径。第三，在每个Transformer模块类别替换后进行类别级恢复蒸馏，减少局部权重重建与组装模型行为之间的不匹配。采用小型8比特保护通道路径作为敏感通道的辅助稳定机制，与BSQ有效载荷分开计量。报告的存储预算包括二进制编码、神经解码器、保护通道有效载荷、LoRA适配器和元数据。

</details>


### [162] [Multi-Modal, Multi-Environment Machine Teaching for Robust Reward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08647)
*Ali Larian,Qian Lin,Chang Zong Wu,Daniel S. Brown*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多环境、多模态反馈下的逆强化学习奖励函数泛化问题，提出了一种分层机器教学算法，通过贪心选择信息丰富的环境并策略性查询低成本反馈，学习跨环境鲁棒的奖励函数，实验结果表明该方法在泛化性能上显著优于基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有逆强化学习（IRL）的最优教学分析局限于单环境、仅示范的设置，忽略了不同反馈模态和环境动态如何共同约束跨环境泛化的奖励函数。由于单一MDP中的示范将奖励信息与环境特定结构纠缠，所学奖励在新环境中往往泛化失败，因此需要探索多环境、多模态的教学方法。

Method: 首先从理论上分析不同反馈模态（如示范、比较等）对奖励函数的约束能力，证明在无限数据条件下比较型反馈比其它模态施加更强的全局约束。在此基础上，提出一种分层机器教学算法：第一步贪心选择能暴露互补奖励约束的信息性环境；第二步在所选环境内策略性地查询低成本反馈（如比较型反馈），以高效学习跨环境鲁棒的奖励函数。

Result: 理论分析表明比较型反馈在无限数据下施加最强的全局约束；实验结果显示，在相同反馈预算下，所提方法相比均匀教学基线取得了显著更低的遗憾值和更强的留出环境泛化能力，验证了多环境、多模态教学对于学习动态鲁棒奖励函数的重要性。

Conclusion: 多环境、多模态的教学方法能够有效学习跨环境泛化的鲁棒奖励函数，克服了传统单环境IRL中奖励与环境结构纠缠导致的泛化失败问题，为自主智能体在复杂动态环境中的行为对齐提供了理论依据和实用算法。

Original Abstract: As autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across diverse operational contexts, aligning their behavior with human intent demands reward functions that remain robust to such changes rather than overfitting to any single environment. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a principled way to infer such objectives from human feedback. However, existing analyses of optimal teaching approaches for IRL focus on single-environment, demonstration-only settings, leaving underexplored how heterogeneous feedback modalities and environment dynamics jointly constrain reward functions that generalize across multiple environments. Because demonstrations in one MDP entangle reward information with that environments specific structure, the resulting rewards frequently fail to generalize when the agent is deployed in a new setting. We first analyze how different feedback modalities constrain rewards, showing that, in the unlimited-data regime, comparisons impose strictly stronger global constraints than other modalities. Beyond this theoretical analysis, we introduce a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm for reward learning that operates across multiple MDPs. The algorithm first greedily selects informative environments that expose complementary reward constraints, then strategically queries low-cost feedback within those environments. Empirically, our method achieves substantially lower regret and stronger generalization to held-out environments than uniform teaching baselines under identical feedback budgets, demonstrating the importance of multi-environment, multi-modal teaching for learning dynamics-robust reward functions.

中文摘要翻译: 随着自主智能体在多样化操作环境中的部署日益广泛，使其行为与人类意图对齐需要奖励函数能够对这些环境变化保持鲁棒，而非过拟合于单一环境。逆强化学习（IRL）提供了一种从人类反馈中推断目标函数的原理性方法。然而，现有关于IRL最优教学方法的分析局限于单环境、仅示范的设置，忽视了异质反馈模态和环境动态如何共同约束能够跨多个环境泛化的奖励函数。由于单个MDP中的示范将奖励信息与该环境的特定结构纠缠在一起，当智能体被部署到新环境时，所得到的奖励函数常常无法泛化。我们首先分析了不同反馈模态对奖励的约束能力，证明在无限数据条件下，比较型反馈比其他模态施加了更强的全局约束。除了这一理论分析，我们提出了一种跨多个MDP进行奖励学习的分层机器教学算法。该算法首先贪心地选择能暴露互补奖励约束的信息性环境，然后在这些环境内策略性地查询低成本反馈。实验结果表明，在相同的反馈预算下，我们的方法相比均匀教学基线取得了显著更低的遗憾值和更强的留出环境泛化能力，这验证了多环境、多模态教学对于学习动态鲁棒奖励函数的重要性。

</details>


### [163] [Secure Decentralized Federated Learning via Gossip and Virtual Voting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08651)
*Amirhossein Taherpour,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出gspDAG-FL，一种从用于模型交换的gossip历史中直接导出共识的去中心化联邦学习框架，通过拓扑有向无环图和Hashgraph式虚拟投票实现最终性，同时通过多层验证机制增强对拜占庭和懒惰节点的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有去中心化联邦学习（DFL）的gossip方法缺乏出处最终性且难以抵御拜占庭或懒惰参与者；而账本辅助的联邦学习虽可审计，但区块链、分片等机制会引入全局协调成本，与DFL的局部性相悖。

Method: 节点仅与邻居交换模型负载；全节点收集事件证书和接收方认可的gossip证明，构建紧凑的拓扑有向无环图（Topology DAG），运行Hashgraph式虚拟投票并生成紧凑的全节点证书。最终性基于唯一的模型来源元组而非参数状态。结合负载验证、认可证明验证和私有语义审计来提升韧性。

Result: 在MNIST分类和Penn Treebank语言建模任务上，网络规模N=100时，gspDAG-FL实现了接近基于验证的账本联邦学习的学习质量，同时减少了协调瓶颈、提高了吞吐量，并在混合拜占庭和懒惰参与场景下保持高异常来源检测率。

Conclusion: gspDAG-FL在去中心化联邦学习中实现了安全、可审计且高效的共识，无需全局协调成本，在保障学习质量的同时提升了系统鲁棒性和吞吐性能。

Original Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) removes the central server by letting nodes exchange model updates through peer-to-peer gossip, but existing gossip-based methods often lack provenance finality and resilience to Byzantine or lazy participants. Ledger-assisted federated learning (FL) improves auditability, yet blockchains, shards, or settlement committees can reintroduce global coordination costs that conflict with DFL locality. This paper proposes \emph{gspDAG-FL}, a secure DFL framework that derives consensus from the same gossip history used to disseminate models. Nodes exchange model payloads only with neighbors, while full nodes collect event certificates and receiver-endorsed accepted gossip proofs, reconstruct a compact Topology directed acyclic graph (DAG), and run Hashgraph-style virtual voting followed by compact full-node certificates. Finality is over unique model-origin tuples, not identical local parameter states. To improve resilience, gspDAG-FL combines payload validation, accepted-proof validation, and private semantic audit before aggregation. We formalize the adversarial setting, prove safety and conditional liveness of the control plane, and give a convergence guarantee for certified perturbed gossip under time-varying effective mixing. Experiments on MNIST classification and Penn Treebank language modeling, using fair held-out validation/audit data and networks up to \(N=100\), show that gspDAG-FL achieves learning quality close to validation-based ledger FL while reducing coordination bottlenecks, improving throughput, and maintaining high invalid-origin detection under mixed Byzantine and lazy participation.

中文摘要翻译: 去中心化联邦学习（DFL）通过让节点以点对点gossip方式交换模型更新来移除中央服务器，但现有基于gossip的方法通常缺乏出处最终性，且难以抵御拜占庭或懒惰参与者。账本辅助的联邦学习（FL）提高了可审计性，但区块链、分片或结算委员会可能重新引入与DFL局部性相冲突的全局协调成本。本文提出\emph{gspDAG-FL}，一种安全的DFL框架，它从用于传播模型的同一gossip历史中推导出共识。节点仅与邻居交换模型负载，而全节点收集事件证书和接收方认可的已接受gossip证明，重构紧凑的拓扑有向无环图（DAG），并运行Hashgraph式虚拟投票，随后生成紧凑的全节点证书。最终性基于唯一的模型来源元组，而非相同的局部参数状态。为提升韧性，gspDAG-FL在聚合前结合了负载验证、认可证明验证和私有语义审计。我们形式化了对抗设置，证明了控制平面的安全性和有条件的活性，并为时变有效混合下的认证扰动gossip给出了收敛性保证。在MNIST分类和Penn Treebank语言建模上的实验，使用公平的留出验证/审计数据以及网络规模N=100，结果表明gspDAG-FL实现了接近基于验证的账本联邦学习的学习质量，同时减少了协调瓶颈，提高了吞吐量，并在混合拜占庭和懒惰参与下保持了高异常来源检测率。

</details>


### [164] [EdgeRefine: Privacy-Utility Balance for Graphs via Jaccard Sampling under Edge Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08659)
*Wenxiu Ding,Muzhi Liu,Zheng Yan,Mingjun Wang,Yifan Zhao,Qiao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EdgeRefine 是一种通过自适应边细化来改善图神经网络边级差分隐私中隐私-效用权衡的局部差分隐私框架。它利用杰卡德相似度估计边存在概率，根据隐私预算控制真假边比例，并采用独立采样率控制总边数，在隐私预算 ε=2.5 时显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 图神经网络在隐私敏感领域应用困难，因为图结构可能泄露敏感链接信息。现有方法通过向邻接矩阵注入噪声实现边级差分隐私，但强隐私需要更多噪声，过度噪声降低效用，隐私-效用平衡成为实际隐私保护图学习的主要障碍。

Method: EdgeRefine 框架首先使用杰卡德相似度估计边存在概率并对边进行排序；然后利用隐私预算 ε 确定真假边的比例，基于概率排序分别采样，并通过独立采样率 k 控制总边数，实现自适应边细化。

Result: 在隐私预算 ε=2.5 下，EdgeRefine 在 ACM 数据集上使用 GAT 架构将节点分类准确率提升 17.8%，在 Cora 数据集上使用 GCN 架构提升 19.7%；图分类任务中相比无噪声基线平均准确率下降约 5%；在图重构攻击下，所有隐私预算下相对绝对误差水平均保持在 1 以上，Cora 平均 1.962，AMAP 平均 1.472。

Conclusion: EdgeRefine 在隐私-效用权衡上显著优于现有隐私保护方法，能够达到与无噪声基线相当的准确率，并且对图重构攻击具有较强的抵御能力，是一种有效的隐私保护图学习框架。

Original Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown considerable success in learning from graph-structured data, but their use in privacy-sensitive areas remains difficult because graph structure can leak sensitive link information. To satisfy edge-level differential privacy, a common approach is to inject noise into all elements of the graph's adjacency matrix, thereby obfuscating the existence of any single edge. However, stronger privacy requires more noise, and excessive noise reduces utility, making the privacy-utility balance a major barrier to practical privacy-preserving graph learning.
  To address this issue, we propose EdgeRefine, a local differential privacy framework that improves this trade-off through adaptive edge refinement. EdgeRefine first estimates edge-existence probabilities using Jaccard similarity and ranks edges for noisy edge removal. To ensure the sparsity and reliability of the final graph, it uses the privacy budget $ε$ to determine the ratio of true to false edges, samples them separately based on this probability ranking, and controls the total number of edges with a separate sampling rate $k$. Extensive experiments show that EdgeRefine achieves accuracy comparable to the noise-free baseline and substantially outperforms other privacy-preserving methods across datasets and GNN architectures. Under privacy budget $ε= 2.5$, EdgeRefine improves node classification accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines by 17.8\% on ACM under GAT and 19.7\% on Cora under GCN. In graph classification, it achieves an average accuracy degradation of around 5\% compared to the noise-free baseline. Under graph reconstruction attacks, EdgeRefine maintains relative absolute error levels above 1 across all privacy budgets, averaging 1.962 on Cora and 1.472 on AMAP, indicating strong resilience against privacy leakage.

中文摘要翻译: 图神经网络（GNN）在处理图结构数据方面取得了显著成功，但它们在隐私敏感领域中的应用仍然困难，因为图结构可能泄露敏感的链接信息。为了满足边级差分隐私，常见的方法是对图邻接矩阵的所有元素注入噪声，从而模糊任意单条边的存在性。然而，更强的隐私保护需要更多的噪声，而过多的噪声会降低效用，使得隐私-效用平衡成为实际隐私保护图学习的主要障碍。针对这一问题，我们提出了 EdgeRefine，一种通过自适应边细化来改善这一权衡的局部差分隐私框架。EdgeRefine 首先使用杰卡德相似度估计边存在概率，并对边进行排序以便进行带噪的边移除。为确保最终图的稀疏性和可靠性，它利用隐私预算 ε 确定真假边的比例，基于该概率排序分别采样，并通过独立的采样率 k 控制总边数。大量实验表明，EdgeRefine 在不同数据集和 GNN 架构上达到了与无噪声基线相当的准确率，并显著优于其他隐私保护方法。在隐私预算 ε=2.5 下，EdgeRefine 在 GAT 架构下的 ACM 数据集上将节点分类准确率提升 17.8%，在 GCN 架构下的 Cora 数据集上提升 19.7%。在图分类任务中，与无噪声基线相比，平均准确率下降约 5%。在图重构攻击下，EdgeRefine 在所有隐私预算下均保持相对绝对误差水平高于 1，在 Cora 上平均为 1.962，在 AMAP 上平均为 1.472，表明其对隐私泄露具有强抵抗力。

</details>


### [165] [Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08665)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型路由中的预算约束问题，提出在线重采样或重路由（RoR）分配策略，在每查询预算和 imperfect verifier 约束下，动态分配预算以最大化期望正确率，实验证明其相比多种基线方法实现了更优的成本-质量帕累托前沿。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有大语言模型路由研究存在两点不足：(1) 单次提交路由器无法达到每个实例最优的改进空间；(2) 先前工作未将重采样已提交模型和重路由到替代模型视为对同一成本预算的竞争性使用方式。因此，本文旨在预算约束下系统地分配重采样与重路由的预算。

Method: 提出在线重采样或重路由（Resample-or-Route, RoR）分配策略。该策略基于每单位成本的边际期望正确率估计，在给定每查询预算和 imperfect verifier 的条件下，动态决策将预算分配给重采样还是重路由，以最大化期望正确率。

Result: 在11个开源模型的池子上、跨4个不同难度的基准测试进行重放实验，结果表明RoR策略相对于单路由、单提交路由器、预算感知的Best-of-K、级联和随机分配等基线方法，实现了更优的成本-质量帕累托前沿；在异质性最高的基准上增益最大；消融实验表明增益受verifier质量门控，当verifier质量下降时增益缩小；鲁棒性实验在提供商价格向量和无标签一致性验证器条件下验证了结论的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 预算约束下的测试时模型选择问题中，将重采样与重路由统一纳入预算分配框架是有效的，所提RoR策略能自适应地在两者之间权衡，取得优于现有方法的成本-质量表现，且该优势依赖于verifier的质量。

Original Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型之间的路由以响应质量换取服务成本，其动机源于已部署路由器与逐个实例最优路由器之间存在报告的性能差距。近期分析表明，测试时重采样可以恢复单次提交路由器无法捕获的逐个实例选择空间；然而，该保证仅在一个理想化的、配备正确性标签且预算无约束的oracle条件下成立，而实际部署系统两者皆不具备。据我们所知，先前没有工作将重采样已提交模型和重路由到替代模型视为对单一每次查询成本预算的竞争性使用。因此，本文提出了预算感知的测试时模型选择问题：给定一个每次查询预算和一个不完美的验证器，将每单位预算分配给重采样或重路由，以使期望正确率最大化。本文提出了一种由每单位成本边际估计正确率驱动的在线重采样或重路由（RoR）分配策略，并将其行为建立在选择与采样之间的可恢复性不对称性之上。在来自11个开源模型池的新重新生成的多轮正确率张量上，跨4个不同难度的基准进行重放实验表明，相对于单路由、单提交路由器、预算感知的Best-of-K、级联和随机分配基线，所提出的RoR策略在测试池中实现了更优的成本-质量帕累托前沿，且在异质性最高的基准上增益最大；消融实验进一步表明，这些增益受验证器质量门控，随着验证器质量下降而缩小；在提供商价格向量和无标签一致性验证器条件下的鲁棒性重放实验描绘了这些结论的适用范围。

</details>


### [166] [A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08690)
*Guoxuan Xia,Luka Ribar,Paul Balanca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了免训练的宽松投机解码技术，统一了现有方法框架，并在当代设置下进行基准测试。关键发现：宽松策略需要大量能力评估，且许多方法依赖质量好的草稿模型，不适合轻量级专用多令牌预测模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准投机解码是无损的（精确保持LLM采样分布），但近期工作认为放宽这一严格保证可以带来更高加速、可控的能力-速度权衡甚至能力提升。本文旨在系统研究这些免训练的宽松投机解码技术。

Method: 将现有免训练宽松投机解码方法统一到共享框架中，在当代设置下进行基准测试，提炼经验发现和实践者指南。

Result: 宽松策略可能需要大量的能力评估（与无损投机解码不同）；许多宽松方法依赖草稿模型本身是好的语言模型，因此不适合轻量级专用多令牌预测草稿模型。

Conclusion: 宽松投机解码并非免费午餐，其适用性受限于草稿模型质量和对能力评估的需求，实践者需谨慎权衡。

Original Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.

中文摘要翻译: 投机解码通过使用更快的辅助模型生成候选令牌，再由LLM并行验证，从而加速自回归LLM的采样过程。标准投机解码是无损的：其拒绝和重采样步骤精确保留了LLM的采样分布。近期研究认为，放宽这一严格保证可以带来更高的加速、可控的能力-速度权衡，甚至能力提升。我们实际研究了免训练的宽松投机解码技术，将现有方法统一到共享框架中，在当代设置下进行基准测试，并提炼出经验发现和实践者指南。重要发现包括：与无损投机解码不同，宽松策略可能需要大量的能力评估；许多宽松方法依赖于草稿模型本身是良好的语言模型，使其不适用于轻量级专用多令牌预测草稿模型。

</details>


### [167] [MPFlow: Learning Budgeted Max-Flow Optimization on the Lightning Network with Deep Graph Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08703)
*Harrison Rush,Vincent Davis,Simone Antonelli,Vikash Singh,Jesse Shrader,Emanuele Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对闪电网络中流动性放置问题，提出图强化学习方法选择最优通道，最大化路由容量（$s$-$t$最大流），在真实网络快照上优于启发式基线，并已部署生产环境执行4640次通道开立决策。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在比特币闪电网络中，节点如何利用固定预算选择开启哪些通道以最大化路由容量，是提升网络效率和资金利用率的关键问题。

Method: 将流动性放置建模为预算约束下的组合优化问题，目标是在图中选择$k$条边加入以最大化$s$-$t$最大流。采用图强化学习，设计消息传递策略网络与PPO算法，结合动作掩码，并在训练中引入枢纽排除课程（移除顶级枢纽节点），迫使策略学习容量感知的放置而非单纯连接枢纽。

Result: 在真实闪电网络快照上的大量实验中，该方法在最大流目标上持续优于多种强启发式基线，且泛化到未见过的图。已部署生产，累计执行4640次通道开立决策，分配267.3 BTC（超1600万美元），覆盖30个托管节点。

Conclusion: 图强化学习方法能有效解决闪电网络中预算约束下的通道选择问题，学习到容量感知的流动性放置策略，并有实际部署价值。

Original Abstract: We address liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network (LN): given a fixed budget, which channels should a node open to maximize its routing capacity? We cast this as a budget-constrained combinatorial optimization problem on graphs, selecting $k$ edge additions that maximize $s$--$t$ max-flow, a theory-grounded measure of routing capacity, and solve it with graph reinforcement learning. Our lightweight agent combines a message-passing policy network with proximal policy optimization (PPO) and action masking, and is trained under a hub-exclusion curriculum: the network's top hubs are removed from training subgraphs, forcing the policy to learn capacity-aware placement rather than hub attachment. In extensive experiments on real Lightning Network snapshots, our method consistently outperforms strong heuristic baselines on the max-flow objective across multiple seeds and unseen graphs. The agent has been deployed in production for peer recommendations, executing 4640 channel-open decisions that cumulatively allocate 267.3 BTC over $16 million across 30 managed nodes.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究比特币闪电网络（LN）中的流动性放置问题：给定固定预算，一个节点应开启哪些通道以最大化其路由容量？我们将此问题建模为图上的预算约束组合优化问题，选择$k$条边加入以最大化$s$-$t$最大流——一种理论有据的路由容量度量，并使用图强化学习求解。我们的轻量级智能体结合了消息传递策略网络与近端策略优化（PPO）及动作掩码，并在枢纽排除课程下训练：从训练子图中移除网络中顶级枢纽节点，迫使策略学习容量感知的放置而非单纯连接枢纽。在真实闪电网络快照的大量实验中，我们的方法在最大流目标上持续优于多种强启发式基线，且泛化到未见过的图。该智能体已部署用于对等节点推荐生产环境，执行了4640次通道开立决策，累计分配267.3 BTC（超1600万美元），覆盖30个托管节点。

</details>


### [168] [Latent Memory Palace: Reasoning for Control as Autoregressive Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08724)
*Chuning Zhu,Eva Xu,Jose Barreiros,Krishnan Srinivasan,Paarth Shah,Abhishek Gupta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜记忆宫殿（LMP）框架，将控制策略中的推理形式化为自回归潜空间上的变分推断，通过迭代自适应检索实现类人推理，在仿真和真实场景中取得优异表现，并衍生出变长动作分词器LMP-tok。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 语言模型展现出自适应推理能力，但将其迁移到连续控制策略中面临挑战——直接在语言空间中推理缺乏空间理解和精确运动所需的细粒度。因此，需要一种能在潜空间中实现类似迭代自适应推理的机制。

Method: LMP将推理形式化为具有自回归潜分布的变分推断，推导出一种潜空间强化学习技术以可优化地最大化其变分下界。由此得到策略LMP-π，同时提出变长动作分词器LMP-tok以提升下游自回归策略性能。

Result: LMP-π在仿真和真实世界领域均取得强实证性能，展现出可解释的、自适应的测试时计算分配；LMP-tok显著提升了自回归策略的性能。

Conclusion: 通过变分推断的视角，为控制问题中的潜空间推理提供了新范式，证明自回归潜空间可以有效组织和检索信息，实现自适应推理。

Original Abstract: Human decision-making is highly flexible -- some actions are taken immediately; others require longer deliberation. Language models have exhibited a similar capacity for adaptive "reasoning." However, transferring this capability to continuous control policies has been challenging, as directly reasoning in language space may lack the granularity for spatial understanding and precise motions. In this work, we show that reasoning for control policies can emerge by organizing information in an autoregressive latent space reminiscent of a memory palace, where retrieval is iterative and adaptive. Our method, Latent Memory Palace (LMP), formulates reasoning as variational inference with an autoregressive latent distribution. We derive a latent-space reinforcement learning technique to tractably optimize its variational lower bound. The resulting policy, LMP-$π$, achieves strong empirical performance in simulation and real-world domains while exhibiting interpretable, adaptive allocation of test-time compute. We further show that the same framework yields a variable-length action tokenizer, LMP-$\texttt{tok}$, which significantly improves the performance of downstream autoregressive policies. Together, these results present a new perspective on latent reasoning for control through the lens of variational inference.

中文摘要翻译: 人类决策具有高度灵活性——有些行为立即执行，另一些则需要更长时间的 deliberation。语言模型已展现出类似的自适应"推理"能力。然而，将这种能力迁移到连续控制策略中一直具有挑战性，因为直接在语言空间中进行推理可能缺乏空间理解和精确运动所需的细粒度。在这项工作中，我们展示控制策略的推理可以通过在以自回归潜空间中组织信息而涌现，该空间类似于记忆宫殿，其中的检索是迭代且自适应的。我们的方法——潜记忆宫殿（LMP）——将推理形式化为具有自回归潜分布的变分推断。我们推导出一种潜空间强化学习技术以可优化地最大化其变分下界。由此得到的策略LMP-π在仿真和真实世界领域展现出强实证性能，同时表现出可解释的、自适应的测试时计算分配。我们进一步展示了相同的框架可以产生变长动作分词器LMP-tok，它显著提升了下游自回归策略的性能。综合来看，这些结果通过变分推断的视角为控制中的潜空间推理提供了新视角。

</details>


### [169] [Super Weights in LLMs and the Failure of Selective Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08733)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Adewale Akinfaderin,Akarsha Sehwag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了"Super Weights"（超大权重）在LLM中的重要性假设，发现移除这些参数并非对所有模型造成灾难性影响，且单独训练Super Weights会导致性能崩溃（降至随机猜测水平），而结构化低秩更新（如LoRA）即使仅更新极少量参数仍能成功。研究表明参数重要性不等同于参数可训练性，有效微调依赖于跨整个层的结构化分解而非孤立地针对重要权重。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 近期工作发现存在"Super Weights"——单个参数被移除后会严重损害模型性能。本文旨在验证这一现象的普适性，并探究这些看似重要的参数是否在训练中同样有效，即检验"参数重要性是否意味着参数可训练性"这一核心假设。

Method: 在OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B上进行系统性消融实验：(1) 单独训练Super Weights（100到8,192个参数）；(2) 扩展到Super Weights的局部邻域（最多36K参数）；(3) 对比训练同等数量的随机位置参数；(4) 使用LoRA进行结构化低秩更新，并对比在Super Weight坐标上施加约束的LoRA。

Result: (1) 单独训练Super Weights（100~8,192参数）导致准确率降至随机猜测水平；(2) 扩展到局部邻域（36K参数）也无改善；(3) 训练同等数量的随机位置参数反而能提升基线性能，说明崩溃是由Super Weights本身导致而非稀疏性；(4) 仅用0.16%参数的Vanilla LoRA在注意力和down_proj层均成功；(5) 在Super Weight坐标上约束LoRA更新与标准LoRA结果无统计显著差异。

Conclusion: 参数的重要性（对模型性能的关键性）并不等价于参数的可训练性（单独训练时的有效性）。有效的微调依赖于跨整个层的结构化分解（如低秩分解），而非孤立地针对个别重要权重进行训练。

Original Abstract: Recent work identified Super Weights, individual parameters whose removal degrades model performance by orders of magnitude. We show that this degradation due to pruning Super Weights does not universally apply to all LLMs. Furthermore, if these parameters are so important, Super Weight-aware training should be effective. We show the opposite. Training Super Weights in isolation (100 to 8,192 parameters) drops accuracy to random-guessing levels on both OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and expanding to local neighborhoods of up to 36K parameters provides no improvement. The failure is specific to Super Weight coordinates: training an equal number of randomly chosen positions in the same down_proj layers instead improves over the baseline, so the collapse comes from targeting Super Weights, not from sparsity itself. Vanilla LoRA, updating every position in attention weight matrices through low-rank structure, succeeds with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same low-rank update to down_proj succeeds as well. A 10-seed ablation confirms that constraining LoRA updates at positions corresponding to Super Weight coordinates yields statistically indistinguishable results. These findings establish that parameter importance does not imply parameter trainability in isolation, and that effective fine-tuning relies on structured decompositions over entire layers rather than targeting individually important weights.

中文摘要翻译: 近期研究发现了"超大权重"（Super Weights）——个别参数的移除会使得模型性能下降数个数量级。我们证明，因剪枝超大权重导致的性能退化并非普遍适用于所有大语言模型。此外，如果这些参数如此重要，那么针对超大权重的训练应是有效的。我们展示了相反的结果。在OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B上，单独训练超大权重（100到8,192个参数）会使准确率降至随机猜测水平，且将训练范围扩展到多达36K参数的局部邻域也未见改善。这种失败是超大权重坐标所特有的：在相同的down_proj层中训练同等数量的随机位置参数反而能提升基线性能，因此性能崩溃是由针对超大权重本身引起的，而非稀疏性所致。原始的LoRA方法通过低秩结构更新注意力权重矩阵中的每个位置，仅用0.16%的参数就取得了成功，且将同样的低秩更新应用于down_proj层也同样成功。一项基于10个种子的消融实验证实，在超大权重坐标对应的位置上对LoRA更新施加约束，所得结果在统计上与标准LoRA无显著差异。这些发现表明，参数的重要性并不等同于参数的单独可训练性，有效的微调依赖于跨整个层的结构化分解，而非针对个别重要权重进行训练。

</details>


### [170] [Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08746)
*Duen Horng Chau,Donghao Ren,Fred Hohman,Dominik Moritz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了UMAP内部构建的kNN图在数据探索中被忽视的潜力，通过在kNN图上直接应用PageRank、k-core分解和聚类系数等经典图算法，能够有效增强数据理解（如识别代表性数据点、发现密集核心区域和紧密邻域），并在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上验证了其与专用方法相比的竞争力与互补性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 典型UMAP工作流仅关注其低维嵌入，完全忽略了UMAP内部构建的kNN图。该kNN图编码了原始高维空间中的数据流形，未受UMAP二维投影带来的畸变影响，具有未被充分挖掘的潜力。

Method: 在UMAP内部生成的kNN图上应用三种经典图算法：(1) PageRank识别代表性数据点；(2) k-core分解揭示密集核心区域与稀疏外围；(3) 聚类系数检测高度相似数据点组成的紧密邻域。

Result: 在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上的定量和定性评估表明，这些基于图的分析不仅实用，而且与专用方法（如用于样本选择的k-medoids、用于密度聚类HDBSCAN）相比具有竞争力或互补性。

Conclusion: UMAP内部kNN图具有巨大的未被利用的潜力，在其上应用标准图算法可以显著增强高维数据的理解与分析能力，是一种与现有专用方法互补的有效途径。

Original Abstract: While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).

中文摘要翻译: 虽然UMAP被广泛用于探索高维数据，但典型工作流仅关注其低维嵌入，很大程度上忽略了UMAP内部构建的丰富k近邻（kNN）图。该kNN图在原始高维空间中编码了数据流形，未受UMAP二维投影引入的畸变影响。我们展示了这一内部表示的未开发潜力，证明了在此图上应用标准图算法可以增强数据理解：（1）PageRank识别代表性数据点，（2）k-core分解揭示密集核心区域与稀疏外围，（3）聚类系数检测高度相似数据点组成的紧密邻域。通过在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上的定量和定性评估，我们表明这些基于图的分析不仅实用，而且与专用方法（如用于样本选择的k-medoids、用于密度聚类的HDBSCAN）相比具有竞争力或互补性。

</details>


### [171] [SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08754)
*David González-Martínez,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 SLORR，一个简单、无状态且保持架构的框架，用于在训练中对神经网络进行低秩正则化，通过基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数的两种变体，在不显著增加训练开销的情况下提升模型的可压缩性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有训练时低秩正则化方法存在以下局限：(1) 需要对大型权重矩阵进行SVD分解，计算开销大；(2) 修改模型架构，引入额外的可训练参数；(3) 依赖有状态的缓存量。为解决这些问题，需要一种简单、无状态、保持架构的低秩正则化方法。

Method: 提出 SLORR 框架，包含两种变体：(1) 基于Hoyer稀疏度量的SLORR-Hoyer；(2) 基于核范数的SLORR-Nuclear。该方法直接对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，使用GPU友好的近似计算前向和反向传播，并给出了近似保证。

Result: (1) 在ImageNet-1K上对ResNet-50、ViT-B/16、ViT-L/16进行短周期继续训练以及对ResNet-18进行预训练，SLORR在引入不到8%训练开销的情况下诱导了可压缩性；(2) 在1.35亿和5.6亿参数的LLM预训练中，SLORR训练的压缩模型性能显著优于未正则化模型，且平均训练开销增加不到1%。

Conclusion: SLORR是一种高效、轻量级的低秩正则化方法，能在不改变模型架构、不增加大量训练开销的前提下有效提升神经网络的可压缩性，适用于从CNN到ViT再到LLM等多种模型架构。

Original Abstract: Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.

中文摘要翻译: 低秩分解被广泛用于压缩神经网络，但现代模型通常难以在不显著损失精度的情况下自然地适应激进的分解。现有的训练时低秩正则化方法可以提升可压缩性，但它们通常需要对大型权重矩阵进行奇异值分解（SVD）、修改模型架构（引入额外的可训练参数），或依赖于有状态的缓存量。为解决这些限制，我们提出了SLORR，一个简单、无状态且保持架构的训练中低秩正则化框架，并基于Hoyer稀疏度量和核范数实例化了两种主要变体。SLORR直接对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，使用GPU友好的近似来计算正则化项的前向和反向传播，我们为此提供了近似保证。我们首先在ImageNet-1K上对ResNet-50、ViT-B/16和ViT-L/16进行短周期继续训练，以及对ResNet-18进行预训练，评估了SLORR的性能，结果表明SLORR在引入不到8%训练开销的情况下诱导了可压缩性。我们进一步在1.35亿和5.6亿参数规模的LLM预训练中评估了SLORR-Hoyer：经过SLORR训练的压缩模型在性能上显著优于未正则化模型，同时平均训练开销增加不到1%。

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [A Non-Hermitian Potential Well Formalism for Conscious--Preconscious--Subliminal Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08302)
*Vasily Lubashevskiy,Ihor Lubashevsky*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 提出了一种全局神经工作空间（GNW）的唯象模型，通过构建复值景观和基于希尔伯特空间的云函数，将早期感觉编码与意识访问统一在一个动力学框架中，使用非线性类薛定谔方程描述了从潜意识到意识的分层处理过程。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 为感觉编码和意识访问提供统一的动力学描述，弥合两者之间的理论鸿沟，并量化地再现潜意识-前意识-意识的分层处理层级。

Method: 构建了一个基于希尔伯特空间的云函数编码高级表征，其动力学由含非厄米、非正则哈密顿量的非线性虚时间类薛定谔方程和Lotka-Volterra型非线性项描述，将哈密顿量的厄米部分和反厄米部分分别对应识别耗散定位和信息广播传播。

Result: 模型成功再现了感觉处理的潜意识-前意识-意识层级；意识访问对应束缚态的出现，只有当GNW景观深度和自上而下注意力均超过阈值时才会发生。

Conclusion: 该框架以可计算的动力学描述将感觉处理、注意力和意识访问统一在一个动力学框架中，为意识研究提供了新的理论工具。

Original Abstract: We propose a phenomenological model of the Global Neuronal Workspace (GNW) in which early sensory processing generates an effective complex-valued landscape governing the dynamics of high-level stimulus representations. This landscape provides a dynamical bridge between sensory encoding and conscious access, enabling both processes to be described within a unified framework. High-level representations are encoded in a cloud function defined on a Hilbert space over a perceptual state space, thereby combining the holistic structure of mental images with a neural implementation. Its dynamics is governed by a nonlinear Schrödinger-type equation in imaginary time with a non-Hermitian, non-normal Hamiltonian and a nonlinear Lotka--Volterra-type term that preserves norm and enables spatially nonlocal interactions. The Hermitian and anti-Hermitian parts of the Hamiltonian generate complementary processes: recognition via dissipative localization at minima of the GNW landscape and information broadcasting via spatial spreading across the state space. The resulting dynamics reproduces the subliminal--preconscious--conscious hierarchy of sensory processing. Conscious access corresponds to the emergence of a bound state, which occurs only when both the GNW landscape depth and the degree of top-down attention exceed threshold values. The resulting framework provides a tractable dynamical description linking sensory processing, attention, and conscious access within a unified dynamical setting.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了一个全局神经工作空间（GNW）的唯象模型，其中早期感觉处理生成一个有效复值景观，该景观支配着高级刺激表征的动力学。这一景观在感觉编码和意识访问之间提供了动力学桥梁，使得两个过程能够在统一框架内被描述。高级表征由定义在知觉状态空间上的希尔伯特空间中的云函数编码，从而将心理意象的整体结构与神经实现相结合。其动力学由含非厄米、非正则哈密顿量的非线性虚时间类薛定谔方程以及一个保持范数并实现空间非局部相互作用的非线性Lotka-Volterra型项共同支配。哈密顿量的厄米部分和反厄米部分生成互补的过程：通过GNW景观最小值处的耗散定位实现识别，以及通过跨越状态空间的空间扩散实现信息广播。由此产生的动力学再现了感觉处理的潜意识-前意识-意识层级。意识访问对应于束缚态的出现，只有当GNW景观深度和自上而下注意力程度均超过阈值时才会发生。由此产生的框架提供了将感觉处理、注意力和意识访问统一在动力学描述中的可计算方法。

</details>


<div id='eess.SP'></div>
